### 4.3 Corridors, Doors, Secret Doors, Spiking (`j`), Bashing & Tunneling (`T`/`+`)

| Feature / Mechanic | VMS Moria 4.8 (`generate.inc`, `moria.inc`) | Umoria 5.7.15 (`dungeon_generate.cpp`, `player.cpp`) | Moria8 (`core/dungeon_features.s`, `core/tunnel.s`, `core/bash.s`) | Verdict | Source Citations |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Room-Wall Penetration Doors (`tunnel` wall stack)** | Each room wall a tunnel steps into is pushed on `wallstk`; after the tunnel, each becomes a door on `randint(100) < dun_tun_pen` (25), else corridor floor. A tunnel that steps into a wall and turns back into the room leaves that wall a door onto unbroken rock | Identical (`dungeonBuildTunnel` `walls_tk`) | `materialize_staged_tunnel` (`core/dungeon_gen.s`) draws the same `rng_range(100) < 24` roll, then places a door only when both wall jambs are wall **and** both cells across the wall are open (tunnel, corridor, room floor, door, or another staged penetration); otherwise the penetration becomes floor. Doors never open onto rock (`docs/DUNGEON_GENERATION_CONTRACT.md` DGN-017, restoring the settled invariant). A turned-back penetration is floor, not a door; levels without one generate identically | **`Approximation`** | `vms-moria: source/include/generate.inc` (`tunnel`)<br>`umoria: src/dungeon_generate.cpp` (`dungeonBuildTunnel`)<br>`moria8: core/dungeon_gen.s` |
| **Door State Generation (`place_door`)** | Roll `randint(3)`: `1..2` closed door (`'`), `3` open door (`'`). For closed doors, `randint(12)`: `1..2` locked (`p1 = randint(10)+10` in `11..20`), `3` stuck (`p1 = -(randint(10)+10)` in `-11..-20`), `4..12` plain (`p1 = 0`) | Exact same probabilities (`2/12` locked `11..20`, `1/12` stuck `-11..-20`, `9/12` plain `0`) | **Exact upstream distribution** in `place_secrets` (`core/dungeon_features.s:264-340`): converts `1..3` closed doors to Secret Doors (`TILE_SECRET`), then rolls `rng_range(120)` on every remaining closed door:<br>- `[0..19]` (`20/120 = 2/12`): **Locked** with difficulty `11..20` (`door_state_val = 11 + roll/2`)<br>- `[60..69]` (`10/120 = 1/12`): **Stuck** with magnitude `11..20` (`door_state_val = $80 | (roll - 49)`)<br>- Otherwise (`90/120 = 9/12`): **Plain** (`0`) | **`Implemented`** | `vms-moria: source/include/generate.inc`<br>`umoria: src/dungeon_generate.cpp`<br>`moria8: core/dungeon_features.s:264-340` |
| **Opening & Picking Locked Doors (`o`)** | Plain door opens (`'`); Locked door (`p1 > 0`) rolls `disarm - p1 > randint(100)`, awards `+1 XP` on unlock; Stuck door (`p1 < 0`) prints *"The door appears to be stuck."* | Identical | **Exact match** in `door_try_open` & `door_pick_roll` (`core/dungeon_features.s:865-961, 1280-1327`):<br>- Refuses if confused (*"You are too confused to pick the lock."*)<br>- Locked (`val > 0`): rolls `1..100 < disarm_skill - lock_diff`; on success awards **+1 XP**, clears state, and opens door in the same turn<br>- Stuck (`val < 0`): prints *"The door appears to be stuck."* | **`Implemented`** | `moria8: core/dungeon_features.s:865-961, 1280-1327` |
| **Closing & Broken Doors (`c`)** | Open door closes (`+`); if broken (`p1 != 0` on open door), prints *"The door appears to be broken."*; if monster in doorway, prints *"The <monster> is in your way!"* (consumes turn) | Identical | **Exact match** in `door_try_close` (`core/dungeon_features.s:967-1045`):<br>- Broken door (`door_state_get == 1` on `TILE_DOOR_OPEN`): prints *"The door appears to be broken."* (consumes turn)<br>- Live monster on tile: prints *"The <monster> is in your way!"* (consumes turn) | **`Implemented`** | `moria8: core/dungeon_features.s:967-1045` |
| **Jamming Doors with Iron Spikes (`j` / `CTRL+D`)** | Requires closed door + Iron Spike (`TV_SPIKE`); sets `p1 = -abs(p1) - 20` (stuck magnitude `+20` per spike) and consumes 1 spike | Identical (`dungeonJamDoor`) | **Exact match** in `door_jam_command` (`core/dungeon_features.s:1148-1277`):<br>- Requires closed unoccupied door + `ITEM_TYPE_IRON_SPIKE` (`ID 135`) in carried pack<br>- Adds `+20` to `|door_state|` (capped at `127`), sets sign bit `$80` (converting locked/plain to stuck), and consumes 1 spike (`inv_consume_one`) | **`Implemented`** | `moria8: core/dungeon_features.s:1148-1277` |
| **Bashing Doors (`B` / `CTRL+B`)** | `bash`: `chance = STR_index + weight/2`; succeeds if `randint(chance*(20+|p1|)) < 10*(chance-|p1|)`; `50%` chance to break door (`p1 = 1 - randint(2)`); steps into doorway; on failure ("The door holds firm.") paralysis is set to `2` (`moria.inc:3445-3457`) | `playerBashClosedDoor`: same formula; on failure, `randint(150) > DEX` paralyses `1 + randint(2)` turns ("You are off-balance.") | **Exact formula** in `bash_door` (`core/bash.s:117-240`):<br>- `chance = zp_player_str + 75` (`75` = typical `weight/2`)<br>- Succeeds iff `rng_range_word(chance * (20 + |s|)) < 10 * (chance - |s|)`<br>- `50%` chance (`rng_range(2) == 0`) to mark door **broken** (`door_state = 1`), steps player into doorway, and runs passive search<br>- On failure, calls `bash_off_balance_door`: Umoria's `randint(150) > DEX` for `1 + randint(2)` turns (until 2026-10-25 `1..2` turns, rolled one lower) | **`Implemented`** | `umoria: src/player.cpp`<br>`moria8: core/bash.s:117-240` |
| **Tunneling / Digging (`T` / `+`)** | Requires digging tool or weapon (`tunnel` p1 * 20 + STR + weapon weight); compares against Granite (`randint(1200)+80`), Magma (`randint(600)+10`), Quartz (`randint(400)+10`), Rubble (`randint(180)`). Drops gold/item if vein had treasure | Identical (`playerTunnel`) | `player_tunnel` (`core/tunnel.s:18-346`):<br>- Confused: `75%` chance random direction<br>- Requires digging tool (`calc_dig_ability`: Shovel/Pick base + `ego` multiplier + `STR`); bare hands print *"You have no tool to dig with."*<br>- Resistance rolls (scaled 1/5th to 8-bit): **Granite/Secret/Closed Door** `rng(240)+16` (`16..255`), **Magma** `rng(120)+5` (`5..124`), **Quartz** `rng(80)+3` (`3..82`), **Rubble** `rng(40)` (`0..39`)<br>- If `FLAG_HAS_ITEM` set on Magma/Quartz vein, spawns gold via `tunnel_spawn_gold` | **`Implemented`** (8-bit scaled resistances) | `umoria: src/player.cpp`<br>`moria8: core/tunnel.s:1-346` |

### 4.4 Line of Sight (LOS), Room Lighting & Infravision

| Feature | VMS Moria 4.8 (`moria.inc`, `dungeon.inc`) | Umoria 5.7.15 (`dungeon_los.cpp`, `dungeon.cpp`) | Moria8 (`core/dungeon_los.s`, `core/los_trace.s`, `docs/TURN_RENDER_CONTRACT.md`) | Verdict | Source Citations |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Three-State Tile Visibility** | `pl` (permanent light), `tl` (temporary light), `fm` (field mark / remembered wall/object) | `lr` (room border/interior), `pl` (permanently lit), `tl` (temporarily lit), `fm` (field mark) | Packed into lower nibble of each map byte:<br>- **Unseen**: `FLAG_VISITED == 0` -> rendered blank<br>- **Visible**: `FLAG_VISITED` and (`FLAG_LIT` or within `zp_light_radius`) -> rendered in full color (or bright `.` on monochrome ports)<br>- **Remembered**: `FLAG_VISITED` and not lit / outside torch radius -> rendered in dark grey `COL_DGREY` (or `' '` on Atari 8-bit & BBC Master monochrome ports) | **`Implemented`** | `moria8: core/dungeon_los.s:1-208`, `docs/TURN_RENDER_CONTRACT.md` |
| **Torch / Lantern Local Illumination** | Lights `3 x 3` (`light_rad=1`) around player, marking adjacent walls `fm=true` | Identical | Phase A of `update_visibility` (`core/dungeon_los.s:72-155`): marks all tiles within Chebyshev distance `zp_light_radius` (`1` for Torch/Lantern) as `FLAG_VISITED` | **`Implemented`** | `moria8: core/dungeon_los.s:72-155` |
| **Lit Room Reveal (`light_room`)** | Stepping onto a `lr` lit room tile reveals the entire room interior and perimeter walls | Identical | Phase B of `update_visibility` (`core/dungeon_los.s:156-202`): when standing on a `FLAG_LIT` tile inside a lit room (`room_lit[x] == 1`), `reveal_room` marks the entire room interior + 1-tile wall border as `FLAG_VISITED` | **`Implemented`** | `moria8: core/dungeon_los.s:156-300` |
| **Tile-Blocked Raycast LOS (`los`)** | `los(y1, x1, y2, x2)`: 32-bit fixed-point / float slope Bresenham raycast checking `fopen` on intermediate cells (max distance `MAX_SIGHT=20`) | `los(y1, x1, y2, x2)`: integer Bresenham slope raycast checking `fopen` (max distance `MAX_SIGHT=20`) | `mm_los_clear_to_target` (`core/los_trace.s:17-193`): integer Bresenham dual-error accumulator (`mlos_adx`, `mlos_ady`, `mlos_steps`) checking `walkable_table` on every intermediate tile, **including diagonal corner-pinch blocking** (`!mlos_side_check+:115-142`: a diagonal step is blocked if both orthogonal side cells are closed walls/doors!) | **`Implemented`** | `vms-moria: source/include/moria.inc`<br>`umoria: src/dungeon_los.cpp`<br>`moria8: core/los_trace.s:1-193` |
| **Infravision (`py.flags.see_infra` / `INF`)** | Races with `infra > 0` (`20..50` ft = `2..5` tiles) see warm-blooded (`not CM_PHASE / undead`) monsters in the dark within `infra` tiles | Same (`infra` in `0..5` tiles; monsters without `CD_NO_INFRA` visible within `see_infra` if `los()` is clear) | `race_properties` stores `INF` (`0, 2, 3, 4, 4, 5, 3, 3` for Human..Half-Troll in `core/tables.s:85-93`); monster visibility in `monster_update_visibility_all` checks torch radius, lit room, `PLF_SEE_INV`/`PFLAG_SEE_INVIS`, `MF_DETECTED`, and clear `mm_los_clear_to_target` | **`Approximation`** | `moria8: core/tables.s:85-93`, `core/dungeon_los.s` |

### 4.5 Complete Floor Trap Catalog (All 18 Traps) & Disarming Mechanics

In VMS Moria 4.8 (`values.inc:2125-2167`) and Umoria 5.7.15 (`data_treasure.cpp:374-391`), there are **18 floor trap templates** (`trap_lista` hidden, `trap_listb` visible `^`). Moria8 (`core/dungeon_data.s:82-100`, `core/dungeon_features.s`, `core/disarm.s`) implements all **18 floor trap types** (`TRAP_TYPE_COUNT = 18`, up to `MAX_TRAPS = 16` per level).

Traps per level follow VMS (`generate.inc:854-866`, `alloc_object([1,2,4],1,randint(alloc_level))` with `alloc_level = trunc(dun_level/3)` clamped to `2..10`): `place_traps` (`core/dungeon_gen.s`) places `rng_range(alloc_level)+1`, so `1-2` traps through DL8 and up to `10` from DL30. Each trap's type is a uniform pick across all `18` templates (`rng_range(TRAP_TYPE_COUNT)`), matching VMS (`randint(max_trapa)`) and Umoria (`randomNumber(MAX_TRAPS) - 1`).

A floor trap is sprung only by a move that puts the player on its square (VMS `move_char` reaches `hit_trap` through `carry` in the moved branch only): `core/game_loop.s` runs `trap_check_at_player` only when `player_move_relocated` is set, as the run path always did.

#### Disarming Mechanics Comparison
- **VMS Moria 4.8 & Umoria 5.7.15**: `tot = disarm_ability - trap_level`; succeeds if `randint(100) <= tot` (awards `trap_xp`, removes trap, moves onto tile); if failed and `randint(tot) > 5` (or `tot <= 5`), sets the trap off!
- **Moria8 (`core/dungeon_features.s`, `core/disarm_helpers.s`, `core/disarm.s`)**: `player_disarm_get_effective_chance` computes `race_disarm + class_disarm + class_disarm_lvl * level + dex_disarm_bonus` (halved/divided by 10 if blinded/no light/confused), subtracts `trap_difficulty[type]`, rolls `rng(100) < chance`:
  - **Success**: prints *"You have disarmed the trap."* (except `TRAP_HIDDEN_OBJECT`, which drops its buried item and prints `HSTR_TUN_FOUND`), awards `trap_disarm_xp[type]`, restores `TILE_FLOOR`, removes trap entry, and steps player onto the tile.
  - **Bad Fail**: prints *"You set the trap off!"*, steps player onto the tile, and triggers `trap_trigger`.

#### Row-by-Row Catalog of All 18 Floor Traps

| # | Trap Name (`trap_listb`) | VMS / Umoria Level (`diff`) | VMS / Umoria Damage & Effect (`hit_trap`) | Moria8 Trap ID & Name | Moria8 Difficulty & Disarm XP | Moria8 Effect (`core/dungeon_features.s:586-934`) | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **1** | **An open pit** | Lvl `1` (`p1=1`) | `2d6` falling damage | **`0`: `TRAP_OPEN_PIT`** (`"an open pit"`) | Diff `5`, `1` XP | Prints *"You fell into a pit!"*, deals **`2d6` damage** (`DEATH_TRAP_PIT`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **2** | **An arrow trap** | Lvl `3` (`p1=2`) | Rolls to-hit vs AC; `1d8` damage | **`1`: `TRAP_ARROW`** (`"an arrow trap"`) | Diff `15`, `3` XP | Prints *"An arrow hits you!"*, deals **`1d8` damage** (`DEATH_TRAP_ARROW`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **3** | **A covered pit** | Lvl `2` (`p1=3`) | `2d6` damage + converts to open pit | **`6`: `TRAP_COVERED_PIT`** (`"an open pit"`) | Diff `10`, `2` XP | Rewrites `trap_type[X]` to `TRAP_OPEN_PIT` and deals **`2d6` damage** (`DEATH_TRAP_PIT`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **4** | **A trap door** | Lvl `5` (`p1=4`) | `2d8` damage + falls to `dun_level + 1` (unless Free Fall) | **`8`: `TRAP_TRAP_DOOR`** (`"a trap door"`) | Diff `20`, `5` XP | Prints *"A trap door opens!"*, deals **`2d8` damage** (`DEATH_TRAP_PIT`), and if alive increments `zp_player_dlvl` (clamped at 99) via `scroll_teleport_level_exec` | **`Implemented`** |
| **5** | **A gas trap (Sleep)** | Lvl `3` (`p1=5`) | Paralyzes player for `randint(4)+4` turns unless Free Action | **`12`: `TRAP_SLEEP_GAS`** | Diff `15`, `3` XP | Prints *"A cloud of gas surrounds you!"*; `PFLAG_FREE_ACT` prints *"You are unaffected."*, else paralyzes `5..8` turns (*"You choke and pass out."*) | **`Approximation`** (shared gas intro) |
| **6** | **A loose rock (Hidden Object)** | Lvl `0` (`p1=6`) | Stepping/disarming reveals a buried floor item | **`15`: `TRAP_HIDDEN_OBJECT`** | Diff `0`, `0` XP | Trigger or disarm removes trap (`TILE_FLOOR`) and stages buried item (`df_stage_hidden_drop`, `md_flags=$11`, `HSTR_TUN_FOUND`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **7** | **A dart trap (Lose STR)** | Lvl `5` (`p1=7`) | To-hit vs AC; `1d4` damage + drains `1` STR | **`7`: `TRAP_STR_DART`** (`"a poison dart"`) | Diff `20`, `6` XP | Prints *"A dart hits you!"*, deals **`1d4` damage** (`DEATH_TRAP_DART`) + drains **`1` STR** (min 3, `HSTR_CHEST_WEAKENED`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **8** | **A strange rune (Teleport)** | Lvl `5` (`p1=8`) | Teleports player to random floor tile | **`3`: `TRAP_TELEPORT`** (`"a teleport trap"`) | Diff `20`, `5` XP | Prints *"You are teleported!"* and moves player to a random walkable floor tile (`trap_teleport`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **9** | **Some loose rock (Rockfall)** | Lvl `5` (`p1=9`) | `2d6` damage + places rubble (`:`) on tile | **`5`: `TRAP_ROCKFALL`** (`"a rockfall trap"`) | Diff `25`, `8` XP | Prints *"You are hit by falling rock."*, deals **`2d6` damage** (`DEATH_TRAP_ROCKFALL`), converts tile to `TILE_RUBBLE` and removes trap (player stays on tile as in VMS) | **`Approximation`** |
| **10** | **A corroded blade** | Lvl `10` (`p1=10`) | `4d6` damage + corrodes armor | **`9`: `TRAP_CORRODE`** (`"a blade"`) | Diff `30`, `10` XP | Prints *"A blade hits you!"*, deals **`4d6` damage** (`DEATH_TRAP_ARROW`), and if alive runs `mon_atk_acid_armour` on worn armor | **`Implemented`** |
| **11** | **A gas trap (Blindness)** | Lvl `5` (`p1=11`) | Blinds player for `randint(50)+25` turns | **`13`: `TRAP_BLIND_GAS`** | Diff `20`, `5` XP | Prints *"A cloud of gas surrounds you!"*, sets `vis_room_revealed = 1`, sets `zp_eff_blind = 26..75` (`HSTR_PIQ_CANT_SEE`) | **`Approximation`** (shared gas intro) |
| **12** | **A gas trap (Confusion)** | Lvl `5` (`p1=12`) | Confuses player for `randint(15)+15` turns | **`14`: `TRAP_CONFUSE_GAS`** | Diff `20`, `5` XP | Prints *"A cloud of gas surrounds you!"*, sets `zp_eff_confuse = 16..30` (`HSTR_PIQ_DIZZY`) | **`Approximation`** (shared gas intro) |
| **13** | **A gas trap (Poison)** | Lvl `5` (`p1=13`) | Poisons player for `randint(20)+10` turns | **`2`: `TRAP_POISON_GAS`** (`"a poison gas trap"`) | Diff `10`, `2` XP | Prints *"A cloud of gas surrounds you!"* and sets `zp_eff_poison = 11..20` (`HSTR_DF_POISONED`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **14** | **A strange rune (Summon Monster)** | Lvl `5` (`p1=14`) | Summons `randint(3)+2` monsters around player | **`16`: `TRAP_SUMMON`** | Diff `25`, `8` XP | Removes rune (`TILE_FLOOR`), stages `3..5` summons in `chest_pending_summons` (`HSTR_PMU_GLYPH_OK`), drained by `chest_run_pending_summons` | **`Implemented`** |
| **15** | **A blackened spot (Fire)** | Lvl `10` (`p1=15`) | `4d6` fire damage (reduced by Resist Fire) | **`10`: `TRAP_FIRE`** (`"a fire trap"`) | Diff `30`, `10` XP | Prints *"Flames hit you!"*, deals **`4d6`** divided by `mm_element_divisor_entry(DF_MS_BREATH_FIRE)` (`DEATH_TRAP_ROCKFALL`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **16** | **A corroded rock (Acid)** | Lvl `10` (`p1=16`) | `4d6` acid damage | **`11`: `TRAP_ACID`** (`"an acid trap"`) | Diff `30`, `10` XP | Prints *"Acid hits you!"*, deals **`4d6`** divided by worn-armour + `PFLAG_RESIST_ACID` divisor (`1..4`, `mon_atk_acid_armour`, `DEATH_TRAP_ROCKFALL`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **17** | **A dart trap (Slow / Lose CON)** | Lvl `5` (`p1=17`) | To-hit vs AC; `1d4` damage + slows player `randint(20)+10` turns (or CON drain in variants) | **`4`: `TRAP_POISON_DART`** (`"a poison dart"`) | Diff `20`, `6` XP | Prints *"A dart hits you!"*, deals **`1d4` damage** (`DEATH_TRAP_DART`) + **50% chance to drain `1` CON** (min 3, `HSTR_DF_CON_DRAIN`) | **`Approximation`** |
| **18** | **A chute (Aggravate)** | Lvl `5` (`p1=18`) | Shouts / wakes monsters within range | **`17`: `TRAP_AGGRAVATE`** | Diff `20`, `5` XP | Prints shriek (`HSTR_MAT_SHRIEK`) and wakes all monsters (`monster_aggravate_all`) | **`Implemented`** |

**Source Citations:** `vms-moria: source/include/values.inc:2125-2167`, `umoria: src/data_treasure.cpp:374-391`, `core/dungeon_data.s:82-100`, `core/dungeon_features.s:58-934`, `core/disarm.s:4-117`

## Section 5: Town, Stores (6 + Black Market + Home), Shopkeepers (All 18) & Haggling

### 5.1 Town Layout, Store Mechanics, Haggling & Pricing

| Feature / Mechanic | VMS Moria 4.8 (`store1.inc`, `store2.inc`) | Umoria 5.7.15 (`store.cpp`, `store_inventory.cpp`) | Moria8 (`core/store.s`, `core/ui_store.s`, `core/ui_home.s`, `core/store_restock_overlay.s`) | Verdict | Source Citations |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Town Buildings & Doors** | **6 stores** (`1` General Store, `2` Armory, `3` Weaponsmith, `4` Temple, `5` Alchemy Shop, `6` Magic-User's Store); each `10 x 5` walls with door symbol `'1'..'6'` | Identical **6 stores** (`'1'..'6'`) | **8 buildings** (`STORE_COUNT = 8`, each `10 x 5` walls):<br>- **`1`** (`0`): **General Store** (`BILBO THE FRIENDLY`)<br>- **`2`** (`1`): **Armory** (`GORN THE ARMORER`)<br>- **`3`** (`2`): **Weaponsmith** (`BRYN THE FORGEMASTER`)<br>- **`4`** (`3`): **Temple** (`GARATH THE HEALER`)<br>- **`5`** (`4`): **Alchemy Shop** (`ELARA THE ALCHEMIST`)<br>- **`6`** (`5`): **Magic Shop** (`ZOLAN THE ENCHANTER`)<br>- **`7`** (`6`): **Black Market** (`THE FENCE`) — **Moria8 addition**<br>- **`8`** (`7`): **Player Home** (`Home`, no owner) — **Moria8 addition** | **`Implemented`** (+2 Moria8 buildings) | `vms-moria: source/include/values.inc:2080-2110`<br>`umoria: src/data_store.cpp`<br>`moria8: core/store_data_defs.s`, `core/store.s:438-455` |
| **Store Inventory Capacity** | `STORE_INVEN_MAX = 24` items per store (2 pages of 12) | `STORE_INVEN_MAX = 24` items per store | `STORE_MAX_ITEMS = 16` slots per building (`a`-`p`, on one screen; `STORE_TOTAL_SLOTS = 128` across all 8 buildings). The list fills rows 3-18; the menu and the gold share row 19.<br>**Changed 2026-11-09:** from `12` slots (`a`-`l`); C64 `$17` saves load into the 16-slot rows (a temporary migration in `platforms/shared/save.s`, to be removed before release) | **`Approximation`** | `moria8: core/store_data_defs.s`, `core/ui_store.s` |
| **Store Restocking (`store_maint`)** | Keeps stock between `store$min_inven (14)` and `store$max_inven (20)` of `store_inven_max (24)` (`constants.inc:70-73`) by deleting `1..4` items and creating `1..4` items from `store_choice[store, 1..26]` | `STORE_MIN_AUTO_SELL_ITEMS (10)` to `STORE_MAX_AUTO_BUY_ITEMS (18)` of `24` (`config.cpp:332-333`), `STORE_TURN_AROUND` turnover | `store_init_all` / `store_restock_all` (`core/store_restock_overlay.s`): keeps VMS's own `STORE_MIN_KEEP (14)` to `STORE_MAX_KEEP (20)`, unscaled; with `16` slots stocking stops when a store is full, so the over-stocked sell-off never runs and a full store turns over. Scaled bounds (`7..10` of `12`, then `9..13` of `16`) until 2026-11-09 drew half the General Store's stock: its few kinds stack, so it sits under the minimum and restocks `MIN + 2 - count` draws a visit (a lantern in stock on `10%`/`15%` of visits against `31%` at VMS's bounds, simulated). Initializes and restocks Stores `0, 3, 4` from full 26-entry VMS `store_choice` tables (`sro_general_choice`, `sro_temple_choice`, `sro_alchemy_choice`, spanning Page 0 and Page 1), Stores `1, 2, 5` by category, and restocks the **Black Market (`6`)** with high-level / enchanted dungeon items. Items that stack (food, potions, scrolls, spikes, flasks of oil, torches with equal fuel, ammunition: the pack's rule, `core/item_stack_macros.s`) join an identical stack in the store, up to `STORE_STACK_MAX = 24`, as Umoria `storeCarryItem`; the turnover sell-off takes one item from a stack and the entry only when it is the last (VMS `store_destroy(..., true)`, `store1.inc`), and an over-stocked store sells whole entries (`store_destroy(..., false)`); Umoria's `storeDestroyItem` takes `randint(count)` instead (followed 2026-10-25 until corrected the same night: it emptied food stacks). Buying takes one from a stack (`inventoryTakeOneItem`).<br>**Fixed 2026-10-25 (M5b playtest):** every stocked item took its own slot with a count of 1 and a purchase took the whole slot, so the General Store held at most a few flasks of oil or rations at a time.<br>**Fixed 2026-10-26 (M5b playtest) & updated Phase 4:** a stocked item comes in the VMS store template's number (`values.inc` store items; `sro_stock_qty` across Page 0 and Page 1): `5` Rations of Food, Hard Biscuits, Strips of Beef Jerky, Wooden Torches and Flasks of Oil, `3` Pints of Fine Ale, Word of Recall and Light, `2` Cure Light Wounds, Identify, Heroism, Magic Mapping, Object Detection, Phase Door, Boldness, Slow Poison, Treasure Detection, Blessing and Detect Invisible, else `1`; Moria8 stocked one of everything before M5b, and a General Store often had a single flask.<br>**Fixed 2026-10-26 (M5d playtest):** ammunition (Arrow, Bolt, Rounded Pebble, Iron Shot) is stocked `24` at a time: VMS `magic_treasure` gives every missile stack `7d6` (`misc.inc:1473-1474`), a store's too (`store_create`, `store1.inc:287`); Moria8 stocks the average at Umoria's `24`-item store cap, a fixed count because the C64 town overlay has no room for the roll (it stocked one at a time until then, so a launcher got about one shot)<br>**Fixed 2026-11-16:** Phase Door is two of the Alchemy shop's `26` choices (`values.inc:1679` `store_choice`, inventory_init `61`; C64 `test_store` Test 48)<br>**Delta:** a sold item joins its stack at the next restock (`store_restock_one`), not at once as in Umoria, since the C64 town overlay has no room for the merge; until then it shows on its own line. A sale into a full store is refused even when it would join a stack (Umoria accepts it); stores with many kinds now sit at `14..16` of `16` items, so a sale is refused more often than in VMS, whose `24` slots leave room above `20` | **`Approximation`** | `moria8: core/store_restock_overlay.s`, `core/store_meta_macros.s`, `core/ui_store.s` |
| **Buy & Sell Price Formulas** | `sell_price`: based on `item_value * chr_adj * rgold_adj[owner_race, player_race]`, capped by `owner.max_cost` | Identical | **Normal Stores (`0..5`)**:<br>- **Buy Ask**: `(base_cost * tool_ego_mult * chr_price_adj[CHR-3] / 100) + p1_bonus` (where `chr_price_adj` is `130%` at CHR 3 down to `100%` at CHR 18, and `p1_bonus` adds `+100 GP` per `+1` hit/dam/AC/ring stat or `+10 GP` per wand/staff charge) (the `base_cost * chr_price_adj` product is 24-bit and all of it is divided by 100; until 2026-10-24 the top byte was dropped, so items over ~500 GP were mispriced at low CHR and the shown price was below the haggle floor, `test_store.s` 49)<br>- **Buy Min Floor**: `base_cost * tool_ego_mult + p1_bonus`<br>- **Sell Max**: `(base_cost * tool_ego_mult * chr_sell_adj[CHR-3] / 100) + p1_bonus` (`25%..50%`)<br>**Black Market (`6`)**:<br>- **Buy**: `base_cost * 3 + p1_bonus` (no haggling)<br>- **Sell**: `base_cost / 10 + p1_bonus` (buys any non-cursed item, no haggling) | **`Implemented`** | `moria8: core/store.s:91-342` |
| **Interactive Multi-Round Haggling (`purchase_haggle` / `sell_haggle`)** | Multi-round numeric bidding between shopkeeper's initial ask/offer and final price; tracks player concessions; insults shopkeeper on lowball/backwards bids; kicks player out of store after `insult_max` insults; auto-skips haggling for items `<= 10 GP` (in Umoria) | Identical (plus auto-accept once enough good bargains are made) | **Full multi-round interactive haggling** in `haggle_buy` & `haggle_sell` (`core/ui_store.s:1057-1502`):<br>- Items `<= 10 GP` or in the Black Market use fast `Y/N` confirmation (`!sb_yn_confirm+:338-355`)<br>- Items `> 10 GP` enter multi-round numeric bidding (`input_read_number`): computes player concession ratio `hg_calc_percent_from_tmp_den` (`5%..15%` step), updates shopkeeper counter-offer (`"HOW ABOUT X GP?"`), clamps at `"FINAL OFFER: X GP"`, and accepts on match/cross!<br>- **Insults & Store Kick**: Backwards or `<5%` concession bids increment `hg_insults` and print a random shopkeeper insult (`hg_show_insult_msg`); **3 insults kick the player out of the store (`hg_kicked[store] = 1`)**! Completing a purchase/sale cools `hg_insults` by 1 (`hg_decrease_insults`) | **`Implemented`** | `vms-moria: source/include/store2.inc`<br>`umoria: src/store.cpp`<br>`moria8: core/ui_store.s:830-1502` |
| **Cursed Item Rejection on Sell** | Shopkeepers refuse to buy cursed items (*"Hmmm, it seems to be cursed."*) | Identical | `!ssell_cat_ok+` (`core/ui_store.s:592-600`) checks `inv_flags & IF_CURSED` and refuses cursed items with error message | **`Implemented`** | `moria8: core/ui_store.s:592-600` |
| **Player Home (`STORE_HOME = 7`)** | Not in VMS Moria 4.8 or Umoria 5.7.15 (added in later Moria derivatives) | Not in Umoria 5.7.15 | **`core/ui_home.s`**: 12 persistent storage slots (`A`-`L`) where the player can stash (`D` Drop/Deposit) and retrieve (`G` Get/Withdraw) items for free across dungeon trips and save files | **`Implemented`** (Moria8 addition) | `moria8: core/ui_home.s`, `core/ui_home_text.s` |

## Section 6: Combat System, Monster Attacks (All 19 Methods & 24 Effects), Status Effects, Chests & Balrog Endgame

### 6.1 Player Melee, Critical Hits, Ranged Fire (`f`) & Throwing (`v`/`T`)

| Mechanic | VMS Moria 4.8 (`moria.inc`) | Umoria 5.7.15 (`player.cpp`, `combat.cpp`) | Moria8 (`core/combat.s`, `core/ranged_fire.s`, `core/throw.s`, `core/bash.s`, `docs/COMBAT_AUDIT.md`) | Verdict | Source Citations |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Player Melee To-Hit Formula (`test_hit`)** | `bth = race.bth + class.bth + level*class.bth_lvl + (ptohit + weapon.tohit)*BTH_PLUS_ADJ(3)`. Hits if `randint(bth) > monster.ac` or `randint(20) == 1` (5% auto-hit) | Identical (`BTH_PLUS_ADJ = 3`, `randint(bth) > ac` or `1/20` auto-hit) | `combat_calc_tohit` / `combat_roll_tohit` (`core/combat.s`):<br>- `chance = class_bth + race_bth` (`+5` Blessed, `+12` Heroism), halved against a target the player cannot see (`MF_VISIBLE`), `+ total_to_hit x 3` (`PL_TOHIT`, the weapon's, Umoria's bare-hands `-3` and too-heavy `STR x 15 - weight`), `+ level x class_bth_per_level`, capped at `255`<br>- `combat_roll_tohit`: a d20 of `1` misses, `20` hits, else `rng_range(chance) >= monster_ac` (`randint(chance) > ac`) | None (the 8-bit linear threshold this row described is gone; row corrected 2026-10-26) | **`Implemented`**
| **Unseen Melee Target** | No reduction: `py_attack` always calls `test_hit(bth, lev, tot_tohit, ac)`; an unseen target is only named "it" | `playerCalculateBaseToHit(monster.lit)`: against a monster the player cannot see, base and level BTH are halved and plus-to-hit counts once instead of ×3 | `player_attack_monster` sets `cmb_target_lit` from the target's `MF_VISIBLE` (MON-001) and `combat_calc_tohit_common` applies Umoria's reduction (`docs/COMBAT_AUDIT.md`); until 2026-10-24 it read the square's `FLAG_LIT` room light, so every corridor fight was at half BTH (`test_combat.s` 42). The attack message does not yet say "it" | **`Implemented`** (Umoria rule; VMS has none) | `vms-moria: source/include/moria.inc` `py_attack`<br>`umoria: src/player.cpp:1114`<br>`moria8: core/combat.s` |
| **Attacks per Round (`attack_blows` 7×6 Table)** | `blows_table[7][6]` indexed by STR/weight ratio `(STR_adj * 10) / weapon_weight` (`<2, <3, <4, <5, <7, <9, >=9`) and DEX bracket (`<10, <19, <68, <108, <118, >=118`), returning **`1..4` blows/turn** | Identical `blows_table[7][6]` | **Exact Umoria `blows_table[7][6]`** (`core/tables.s:218-240`, `core/combat.s:249-330`): computes `adj_weight = (STR * 10) / max(weapon_weight, 1)`, indexes `blow_weight_thresholds` (`2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 9`) and `blow_dex_thresholds` (`10, 19, 68, 108, 118`), returning **`1..4` blows/round** (unarmed fixed to `1` blow) | **`Implemented`** | `vms-moria: source/include/moria.inc`<br>`umoria: src/player.cpp`<br>`moria8: core/tables.s:218-240`, `core/combat.s:249-330` |

Ammunition comes in `7d6` stacks in the dungeon (`item_spawn_level`, VMS `magic_treasure`; `5..10` until 2026-10-26) and `24` in stores (store restocking row).<br>**Delta (2026-10-26, M5d playtest; maintainer: Umoria's damage as a stated fix, VMS otherwise):** until then range `19` for every launcher, no distance penalty, no critical hit, no launcher bonus (damage was ammunition dice + `PL_TODMG`), ammunition and launcher to-hit not added, and every shot lost. Remaining deltas: a missed shot does not look for a free square in the 3x3 around (it may share a square with another floor item, as a thrown item does) and no "disappears" message; the critical-hit chance uses the melee per-level gain and to-hit (`combat_critical_blow_weight`); no unlit-target penalty (VMS). The Long Bow, Composite Bow and Heavy Crossbow were added 2026-10-26 as IDs `137-139` (`it_missile2`, `IT_MISSILE2_BASE`) | **`Approximation`** | `vms-moria: source/include/moria.inc:3163-3360`<br>`umoria: src/player_throw.cpp:26-110, 116-150, 180-275`<br>`moria8: core/ranged_fire.s, core/throw.s (tw_save_slot, tw_drop_saved), core/projectile.s` |
| **Throwing Any Item (`v` / `SHIFT+T`)** | `throw_object`/`facts`/`drop_throw` (`moria.inc:3163-3360`): throws any pack item up to `min(10, (STR + 20) * 10 / weight)` squares; to-hit `0.75 x bthb` less one per square flown, with `ptohit + item tohit`; damage the item's dice plus its own `todam` (`2d6` fire for a Flask of Oil), then `critical_blow(weight, ...)`; an item that hits is used up, one that misses lands at the last open square or within the 3x3 around it when `randint(10) > 1`, else "disappears" | Same (`playerThrowItem`); Umoria also takes the wielded weapon's to-hit back out of the throw | `throw_item` (`core/throw.s`): the same range (`projectile.s`); to-hit `combat_calc_tohit_common` (missile skill, race, `3 x (PL_TOHIT + item to-hit)`, level) at 75%, less the squares flown (`throw_calc_tohit`, `rf_dist`); damage the item's dice (`it_dmg_packed`; `1d1` for a `0d0` item) plus the STR damage bonus and the item's own to-dam, floored at 0 (`tw_roll_damage`), then `combat_critical_blow_weight` by its weight; an item that hits is used up (`tw_hit_used_up`); one that misses lands on the last open square of its path when `rng_range(10) != 0` (`tw_consume_item`, `tw_drop_saved`); a potion always shatters.<br>**Delta (2026-10-26, M5d; maintainer: VMS rules with Moria8's improvements kept):** until then a thrown item always landed, hit or miss, its damage added `PL_TODMG` (the wielded weapon's and rings' to-dam), and it had no item to-hit, distance penalty or critical hit. **Kept as intentional Moria8 additions:** the STR damage bonus on a throw (VMS adds none), `1d1` for a `0d0` item (VMS `0`), and potions shattering (VMS lands them). Remaining deltas: the flask's `2d6` is untyped, not fire (no fire vulnerability or resistance); a miss does not look for a free square in the 3x3 around and prints no "disappears" | **`Approximation`** | `vms-moria: source/include/moria.inc:3163-3360`<br>`moria8: core/throw.s` |

### 6.2 Monster Melee, AC Reduction & Attack Execution

| Mechanic | VMS Moria 4.8 (`creatures.inc`) | Umoria 5.7.15 (`monster.cpp`) | Moria8 (`core/monster_attack.s`, `core/monster_attack_effects.s`) | Verdict | Source Citations |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Attacks per Monster Turn** | Up to **4 attacks** (`c_list[i].damage[1..4]` indexing `monster_attacks[0..214]`) | Up to **4 attacks** (`attack[0..3]` indexing `monster_attacks[0..214]`) | Up to **4 attacks** per monster template (`cr_atk0_*`, `cr_atk1_*`, `cr_atk2_*`, `cr_atk3_*`, unpacked via `mon_atk_next_slot` in `core/monster_attack.s`, `MON-032`, `2026-11-16`). `ATK_NONE (0)` ends the attack loop (slot 0 `ATK_NONE` = Quylthulg / Rotting Quylthulg makes no melee round, message, or recall count); `ATK_BLANK (99)` skips an empty slot; `ATK_AGGRAVATE (20)` always hits without a to-hit roll. | **`Implemented`** | `vms-moria: source/include/creature.inc` (`make_attack`), `values.inc` `c_list`<br>`umoria: src/monster.cpp`<br>`moria8: core/monster_attack.s:54-162`, `platforms/shared/monster_catalog/gen_monster_catalog.py` |
| **Monster To-Hit Formula** | `test_hit(bth = effect_base_bth + monster_level * 3, player_total_ac)` (`5%` auto-hit, `5%` auto-miss); theft (`12`, `13`) rolls against `player_level` instead of `player_total_ac` (`creature.inc:318-323`) | Identical | `mon_atk_calc_tohit` / `mon_atk_roll_tohit` (`core/monster_attack.s:256-318`): signed `chance = mon_atk_base_tohit[atk_type] + cr_level * 3` clamped to `[0, 255]` (`ATK_LOSE_STR` uses signed `bth = -3`); a d20 of `1` misses, `20` hits, else `rng_range(chance) >= target`, where `target` is `zp_player_lvl` for `ATK_STEAL_GOLD (12)` / `ATK_STEAL_ITEM (13)` and `mon_atk_effective_ac` (`zp_player_ac`, `+2` while Blessed) for all other types; `Protection from Evil` repels `CF_EVIL` attackers with `cr_level <= zp_player_lvl` before any roll. | **`Implemented`** | `vms-moria: source/include/creature.inc:296-346`<br>`moria8: core/monster_attack.s:15-41, 128-139, 256-318` |
| **Player AC Damage Reduction** | Only the Normal attack (type `1`) is reduced: `dam := dam - round(((pac+ptoac)/200.0)*dam)`, rounded to nearest integer; every other attack effect takes its full `damroll` (`creature.inc:385-390`) | Only case `1` (Normal): `damage -= ((py.misc.ac + py.misc.magical_ac) * damage) / 200`, integer division (`monster.cpp:1547-1550`) | `mon_atk_ac_reduce` (`core/monster_attack.s`): `damage -= (AC * damage) / 200`, 16-bit product (`math_multiply`) truncated by `math_div_16x8` (as Umoria), floored at `0`, with `AC = mon_atk_effective_ac` (`zp_player_ac`, `+2` while Blessed). Applied on **`ATK_NORMAL (1)`, `ATK_BLIND (10)`, and the 5 stat-drain attacks (`ATK_LOSE_STR/DEX/CON/INT/WIS`: `2, 15..18`)** (`core/monster_attack.s`, `core/monster_attack_effects.s:171`); `ATK_CONFUSE (3)`, `ATK_FEAR (4)`, `ATK_PARALYZE (11)`, `ATK_POISON (14)`, `ATK_CORRODE (9)`, and elemental touches (`ATK_FIRE/ACID/COLD/LIGHT`: `5..8`) apply unreduced dice (`0` HP on non-HP touches `12, 13, 19..24`). | **`Approximation`** (Umoria truncation formula; also AC-reduces `ATK_BLIND` & `ATK_LOSE_*`) | `vms-moria: source/include/creature.inc:385-390`<br>`umoria: src/monster.cpp:1547-1550`<br>`moria8: core/monster_attack.s`, `core/monster_attack_effects.s:163-172` |
| **Glyph of Warding Protection** | Monster attacking a player standing on a Glyph of Warding (`;`) must roll `randint(OBJ_RUNE_PROT=70) < monster.level`; if it succeeds, the glyph breaks; otherwise the attack is blocked! | Identical | **Implemented** in `monster_ai.s` / `monster_attack.s` (`glyph_find_at` in `core/item.s:390-440`): monsters cannot step onto an active Glyph of Warding unless `rng_range(70) < cr_level`, which breaks the glyph (`glyph_remove`) and prints *"The rune of protection is broken!"* | **`Implemented`** | `moria8: core/item.s:390-440`, `core/monster_ai.s` |

### 6.3 Complete Monster Attack Method Catalog (All 19 Attack Descriptions)

In VMS Moria 4.8 and Umoria 5.7.15 every monster attack carries a description (`adesc` / `description_id`: `1..19`, plus `99` for a blow repelled by Protection from Evil; VMS `creature.inc:351-383`, Umoria `monster.cpp:256-353`). **Moria8 implements all 19 VMS `ADESC_*` attack verbs (`2026-11-16`, Phase 5, `MON-032`):** `gen_monster_catalog.py` packs the 5-bit `ADESC_*` ID across `sides[7:4]:type[7]` (`type[6:0]` holds `ATK_*`), unpacked by `mon_atk_next_slot` (`core/monster_attack.s:70-92`). Under Moria8's single-summary-per-round cadence (`MON-019`), any connecting blow increments `mat_any_hit` (including `0d0` touches) and records `mat_hit_verb` (preferring specific verbs `2..19` over `ADESC_HITS = 1`); `ADESC_HITS (1)` decodes `HSTR_MAT_HITS` and `ADESC 2..19` unpack a compact 5-bit phrase table via `mon_atk_verb_to_ptr2` (`core/monster_attack_effects.s:17-105`) to print `"The <name> <verb>"` (or `"The <name> misses you."` when all blows miss).

| Attack Type ID | VMS Moria 4.8 & Umoria 5.7.15 Verb (`attack_desc`) | Moria8 Mapping (`core/monster_attack.s`, `core/monster_attack_effects.s`) | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **`1`** | `"hits you."` (VMS `:352`; Umoria `:258`) | **`ADESC_HITS (1)`**: `"The <name> hits you."` (`HSTR_MAT_HITS`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **`2`** | `"bites you."` (VMS `:353`; Umoria `:261`) | **`ADESC_BITES (2)`**: `"The <name> bites you."` (`mon_atk_verb_to_ptr2`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **`3`** | `"claws you."` (VMS `:354`; Umoria `:264`) | **`ADESC_CLAWS (3)`**: `"The <name> claws you."` (`mon_atk_verb_to_ptr2`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **`4`** | `"stings you."` (VMS `:355`; Umoria `:267`) | **`ADESC_STINGS (4)`**: `"The <name> stings you."` (`mon_atk_verb_to_ptr2`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **`5`** | `"touches you."` (VMS `:356`; Umoria `:270`) | **`ADESC_TOUCHES (5)`**: `"The <name> touches you."` (`mon_atk_verb_to_ptr2`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **`6`** | `"kicks you."` (VMS `:357`; compiled out in Umoria `:273-277`) | **`ADESC_KICKS (6)`**: `"The <name> kicks you."` (`mon_atk_verb_to_ptr2`, VMS exact) | **`Implemented`** |
| **`7`** | `"gazes at you."` (VMS `:358`; Umoria `:278`) | **`ADESC_GAZES (7)`**: `"The <name> gazes at you."` (`mon_atk_verb_to_ptr2`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **`8`** | `"breathes on you."` (VMS `:359`; Umoria `:281`) | **`ADESC_BREATHES (8)`**: `"The <name> breathes on you."` (`mon_atk_verb_to_ptr2`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **`9`** | `"spits on you."` (VMS `:360`; Umoria `:284`) | **`ADESC_SPITS (9)`**: `"The <name> spits on you."` (`mon_atk_verb_to_ptr2`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **`10`** | `"makes a horrible wail."` (VMS `:361`; Umoria `:287`) | **`ADESC_WAILS (10)`**: `"The <name> makes a horrible wail."` (`mon_atk_verb_to_ptr2`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **`11`** | `"embraces you."` (VMS `:362`; compiled out in Umoria `:290-294`) | **`ADESC_EMBRACES (11)`**: `"The <name> embraces you."` (`mon_atk_verb_to_ptr2`, VMS exact) | **`Implemented`** |
| **`12`** | `"crawls on you."` (VMS `:363`; Umoria `:295`) | **`ADESC_CRAWLS (12)`**: `"The <name> crawls on you."` (`mon_atk_verb_to_ptr2`) | **`Implemented`** |

### 6.4 Complete Monster Attack Effect Catalog (All 24 Attack Effects)

Every monster attack in VMS Moria 4.8 and Umoria 5.7.15 has an effect ID (`atype` / `type_id`: `1..24`) selecting its base to-hit (`mon_atk_base_tohit[1..24]` in `core/monster_attack.s:15-41`) and on-hit handler (`mon_atk_effect_dispatch` in `core/monster_attack.s` and `mon_atk_effect_ext` / `mae_cold_body` in `core/monster_attack_effects.s`). **As of Phase 5 (`2026-11-16`, `MON-032`), Moria8 implements all 24 VMS `ATK_*` attack effects across all 7 ports + 5 cartridges** with exact VMS base-to-hit values (including signed `-3` on `ATK_LOSE_STR` and `zp_player_lvl` target on theft `12/13`).

| Effect ID | VMS Moria 4.8 & Umoria 5.7.15 Effect (`attack_id` + Base BTH) | VMS / Umoria Mechanics | Moria8 Effect Constant & Implementation (`core/monster_attack.s`, `core/monster_attack_effects.s`) | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **`1`** | **Normal Damage** (`BTH 60`) | `dam - round((AC + to-AC) * dam / 200)`, then HP damage (`creature.inc:385-391`; Umoria truncates, `monster.cpp:1547-1551`) | **`ATK_NORMAL (1)`**, base `60`: `mon_atk_ac_reduce` subtracts `(AC * dam) / 200` (truncated, floor `0`) with `mon_atk_effective_ac` (`+2` while Blessed), then `mon_atk_apply_damage` | **`Implemented`** |
| **`2`** | **Lose Strength (`STR`)** (`BTH -3`) | Full damage; Sustain STR blocks, else `50%` (`randint(2) = 1`) lowers STR (`de_statp`) | **`ATK_LOSE_STR (2)`**, signed base `-3`: AC-reduced HP damage (`mae_lose_str`, `core/monster_attack_effects.s:163-194`); `SUST_STR` in `player_sust_mask` prints *"You are unaffected."*, else `50%` roll lowers `PL_STR_CUR` via `decrement_stat`, recalculates combat/HP, and prints drain feedback | **`Implemented`** |
| **`3`** | **Confusion** (`BTH 10`) | Full damage; `50%` (`randint(2) = 1`) sets `confused += randint(level) + 3` (or `+3` if already confused); no save | **`ATK_CONFUSE (3)`**, base `10`: full dice damage; `50%` roll (`mon_atk_effect_confuse`) sets `zp_eff_confuse` to `randint(level) + 3` (*"The `<name>` confuses you."*) or adds `+3` (capped at `255`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **`4`** | **Terrify (Fear)** (`BTH 10`) | Full damage; WIS save resists, else `afraid += 3 + randint(level)` (or `+3` if already afraid) | **`ATK_FEAR (4)`**, base `10`: full dice damage; blocked by Heroism or WIS-adjusted `player_saving_throw`, else sets `eff_fear_timer` to `3 + randint(level)` (*"The `<name>` frightens you."*) or adds `+3` | **`Implemented`** |
| **`5`** | **Fire Damage** (`BTH 10`) | `fire_dam`: full dice, `div 3` per fire resistance (`1/9` stacked); pack items can burn | **`ATK_FIRE (5)`**, base `10`: *"The `<name>` burns you."*, full dice divided by `mm_element_divisor` (`div 3` for `PFLAG_RESIST_FIRE`, `div 3` again for `zp_eff_resist`; pack items do not burn) | **`Implemented`** (no pack burn) |
| **`6`** | **Acid** (`BTH 0`) | `acid_dam`: `minus_ac` on 1 random worn armour piece (halving damage), `div 3` for acid resist (`div 4` with both) | **`ATK_ACID (6)`**, base `0` (`core/monster_acid.s`): *"The `<name>` spits acid on you."*, picks 1 worn piece across body/shield/head/hands/boots/cloak (`minus_ac` `-1` unless acid-resistant, halving damage), plus `PFLAG_RESIST_ACID` (`div 3`, or `div 4` with armour; no pack damage) | **`Implemented`** (no pack damage) |
| **`7`** | **Cold Damage** (`BTH 10`) | `cold_dam`: full dice, `div 3` per cold resistance (`1/9` stacked); potions can shatter | **`ATK_COLD (7)`**, base `10`: *"The `<name>` freezes you."*, full dice divided by `mm_element_divisor` (`div 3` for `PFLAG_RESIST_COLD`, `div 3` again for `eff_resist_cold_timer`; potions do not shatter) | **`Implemented`** (no potion shatter) |
| **`8`** | **Lightning** (`BTH 10`) | `light_dam`: full dice, `div 3` with lightning resistance | **`ATK_LIGHT (8)`**, base `10`: *"The `<name>` shocks you."*, full dice divided by `3` under `PFLAG_RESIST_LIGHT` | **`Implemented`** |
| **`9`** | **Corrosion** (`BTH 0`) | `corrode_gas`: `minus_ac` on 1 random worn armour piece, else `+randint(8)` HP damage, plus full dice | **`ATK_CORRODE (9)`**, base `0` (`mae_corrode`, `core/monster_attack_effects.s:140-154`): runs `mon_atk_acid_armour` (`minus_ac` on 1 random worn armour piece), else adds `+1d8` to unreduced dice damage | **`Implemented`** (no pack corrosion) |
| **`10`** | **Blindness** (`BTH 2`) | Full damage; `blind += 10 + randint(level)` (`+5` stack) | **`ATK_BLIND (10)`**, base `2` (`mon_atk_effect_blind`, `core/monster_attack.s`): AC-reduced dice damage; sets `zp_eff_blind` to `11 + randint(level)` (or adds `+5` when already blind, capped at `255`, setting `vis_room_revealed = 1`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **`11`** | **Paralyze** (`BTH 2`) | Full damage; CON save or Free Action resists, else `paralysis := randint(level) + 3` | **`ATK_PARALYZE (11)`**, base `2`: full dice damage; CON-adjusted `player_saving_throw` or `PFLAG_FREE_ACT` (*"You are unaffected."*) blocks, else sets `zp_eff_paralyze = randint(level) + 3` | **`Implemented`** |
| **`12`** | **Steal Money** (`BTH 5` vs `player_level`) | Unless unparalyzed & `randint(124) < DEX`, steals `au/10 + randint(25)` gold; `50%` blink away; `0` HP | **`ATK_STEAL_GOLD (12)`**, base `5` vs `zp_player_lvl` (`mae_steal`, `core/monster_attack_effects.s:386-443`): `0` HP; if paralyzed or `randint(124) >= zp_player_dex`, subtracts `(PL_GOLD div 10) + randint(25)` from 24-bit gold; rolls `50%` to blink thief to `find_random_floor` | **`Implemented`** |
| **`13`** | **Steal Object** (`BTH 2` vs `player_level`) | Unless unparalyzed & `randint(124) < DEX`, steals 1 random pack item; `50%` blink away; `0` HP | **`ATK_STEAL_ITEM (13)`**, base `2` vs `zp_player_lvl` (`mae_steal`): `0` HP; if paralyzed or `randint(124) >= zp_player_dex`, consumes 1 uniform random occupied pack slot (`mae_pick_slot`); rolls `50%` to blink thief away | **`Implemented`** |
| **`14`** | **Poison** (`BTH 5`) | Full damage; `poisoned += randint(level) + 5` | **`ATK_POISON (14)`**, base `5`: full dice damage; adds `randint(level) + 5` turns to `zp_eff_poison` (capped at `255`), printing *"The `<name>` poisons you."* when newly poisoned | **`Implemented`** |
| **`15`** | **Lose Dexterity (`DEX`)** (`BTH 0`) | Full damage; Sustain DEX blocks, else lowers DEX | **`ATK_LOSE_DEX (15)`**, base `0` (`mae_lose_stat`): AC-reduced HP damage; `SUST_DEX` blocks (*"You are unaffected."*), else lowers `PL_DEX_CUR` via `decrement_stat` and recalculates combat/HP | **`Implemented`** |
| **`16`** | **Lose Constitution (`CON`)** (`BTH 0`) | Full damage; Sustain CON blocks, else lowers CON | **`ATK_LOSE_CON (16)`**, base `0` (`mae_lose_stat`): AC-reduced HP damage; `SUST_CON` blocks, else lowers `PL_CON_CUR` via `decrement_stat` and recalculates combat/HP (clamping `HP <= MHP`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **`17`** | **Lose Intelligence (`INT`)** (`BTH 2`) | Full damage; Sustain INT blocks, else lowers INT | **`ATK_LOSE_INT (17)`**, base `2` (`mae_lose_stat`): AC-reduced HP damage; `SUST_INT` blocks, else lowers `PL_INT_CUR` via `decrement_stat` | **`Implemented`** |
| **`18`** | **Lose Wisdom (`WIS`)** (`BTH 0` VMS) | Full damage; Sustain WIS blocks, else lowers WIS | **`ATK_LOSE_WIS (18)`**, base `0` (`mae_lose_stat`, VMS exact): AC-reduced HP damage; `SUST_WIS` blocks, else lowers `PL_WIS_CUR` via `decrement_stat` | **`Implemented`** |
| **`19`** | **Lose Experience (`EXP`)** (`BTH 5`) | `lose_exp(damroll(dice) + 2 * (exp div 100))`; `0` HP damage | **`ATK_LOSE_EXP (19)`**, base `5` (`mae_lose_exp`, `core/monster_attack_effects.s:459-495`): `0` HP; subtracts `2 * (PL_XP div 100) + damroll(dice)` from 24-bit `PL_XP_0..2` (floor `0`) and drops `PL_LEVEL` / recalculates HP & combat when below threshold | **`Implemented`** |
| **`20`** | **Aggravate Monsters** (always hits) | Shrieks, waking monsters (`aggravate_monster`); `0` HP damage | **`ATK_AGGRAVATE (20)`**: always hits without a to-hit roll (`core/monster_attack.s:145-146, 202-205`), prints *"A high-pitched shriek echoes!"*, and wakes all monsters (`monster_aggravate_all`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **`21`** | **Disenchant** (`BTH 20`) | Lowers positive `tohit`, `todam`, `toac` of 1 random worn equipment piece by `randint(2)` each; `0` HP | **`ATK_DISENCHANT (21)`**, base `20` (`mae_disenchant`, `core/monster_attack_effects.s:505-545`): `0` HP; picks 1 random slot in `{WEAPON, BODY, SHIELD, HEAD, HANDS, FEET, CLOAK}`, subtracts `randint(2)` (floored at `0`) from positive `to_hit`/`to_dam`/`to_ac`, and runs `player_recalc_equipment` | **`Implemented`** |
| **`22`** | **Eat Food** (`BTH 5`) | Destroys 1 random food item in pack; `0` HP | **`ATK_EAT_FOOD (22)`**, base `5` (`mae_eat_food`, `core/monster_attack_effects.s:551-557`): `0` HP; picks 1 uniform random `ICAT_FOOD` pack slot (`mae_pick_slot`) and consumes 1 unit (`inv_consume_one`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **`23`** | **Eat Light** (`BTH 5`) | Drains `250 + randint(250)` turns of light fuel; `0` HP | **`ATK_EAT_LIGHT (23)`**, base `5` (`mae_eat_light`, `core/monster_attack_effects.s:559-579`): `0` HP; drains `8..16` charges (`240..480` turns at `30` turns/charge, floored at `1` charge) from equipped `EQUIP_LIGHT` and prints *"Your light is growing dim."* | **`Implemented`** |
| **`24`** | **Eat Charges** (`BTH 15`) | Zeroes charges of 1 random pack wand/staff and heals monster `level * charges`; `0` HP | **`ATK_EAT_CHARGES (24)`**, base `15` (`mae_eat_charges`, `core/monster_attack_effects.s:581-612`): `0` HP; picks 1 uniform random charged (`inv_p1 > 0`) pack wand/staff, zeroes `inv_p1`, and heals attacker `cr_level * charges` HP (clamped at `$7FFF`) | **`Implemented`** |

**Source Citations:** `vms-moria: source/include/creature.inc:242-662`, `source/include/moria.inc:1672-1748`<br>`umoria: src/monster.cpp:256-353, 384-470, 1541-1745`, `src/player.cpp:262-366`<br>`moria8: core/monster_attack.s`, `core/monster_attack_effects.s`, `core/monster_acid.s`, `core/monster.s`, `platforms/shared/monster_catalog/gen_monster_catalog.py`

Scroll (`irs_effect_protect`) and prayer (`eff_protect_from_evil`) add `randint(25) + 3*lev`, saturating at `255`; `turn_tick_effects` counts it down silently. | **`Implemented`** | `vms-moria: source/include/creature.inc` `make_attack` (attack type 99), `spells.inc` `protect_evil`, `scrolls.inc` case 28, `prayer.inc` case 21<br>`umoria: src/monster.cpp` `monsterAttackPlayer`, `src/player_magic.cpp` `playerProtectEvil`<br>`moria8: core/monster_attack.s`, `core/item_actions_overlay.s`, `core/player_magic_execute_overlay.s`; C64 `test_monster_attack.s` 23-26, `test_timed_buffs.s` 7-10 |

### 6.6 Chests, Chest Traps (All 6 Flags) & Monster Death Loot-Drop Mechanics

#### A. Chests & Chest Traps (`core/chest.s`, `core/chest_loot.s`, `core/chest_search.s`, `docs/CHEST_DESIGN.md`)

In VMS Moria 4.8 (`values.inc`, `moria.inc`) and Umoria 5.7.15 (`data_treasure.cpp`, `player.cpp`), there are **6 chest types** (`Small/Large Wooden`, `Small/Large Iron`, `Small/Large Steel`) plus `& ruined chest`. Every chest has a lock + trap bitmask in `flags` (rolled via `randint(depth) + 4` across 18 bands) and drops treasure (`p1` iterations of `place_object` at the chest's source level) when opened!

**Moria8 implements the complete VMS/Umoria chest lifecycle** across all 7 ports (`core/item_defs.s:109-129`, `core/item.s:1697-1736`, `core/chest.s`, `core/chest_loot.s`, `core/chest_search.s`, `core/chest_summons.s`):
- **All 7 Chest Item Templates (`IDs 128..134`)**:
  - `128`: `Small Wooden Chest` (min DL `7`, treasure bits `$18`: `90%` + `1d2` drops)
  - `129`: `Large Wooden Chest` (min DL `15`, treasure bits `$24`: `60%` + `2d2` drops)
  - `130`: `Small Iron Chest` (min DL `25`, treasure bits `$30`: `1d2` + `2d2` drops)
  - `131`: `Large Iron Chest` (min DL `35`, treasure bits `$28`: `90%` + `2d2` drops)
  - `132`: `Small Steel Chest` (min DL `45`, treasure bits `$34`: `60%` + `1d2` + `2d2` drops)
  - `133`: `Large Steel Chest` (min DL `50`, treasure bits `$38`: `90%` + `1d2` + `2d2` drops)
  - `134`: `Ruined Chest` (cost `0`, created when a locked chest is bashed open)
- **All 6 Chest Trap & Lock Flags (`inv_p1` / `fi_p1` bits `0..7`)**:
  - `CHEST_P1_LOCKED ($01)`: Locked; picked via `o` (`chest_open_command` rolling `chest_disarm_skill - source_level > rng(100)+1`).
  - `CHEST_P1_TRAP_STR ($02)`: Poison needle — drains `1` STR (`decrement_stat`) + `1d4` damage.
  - `CHEST_P1_TRAP_POISON ($04)`: Poison needle — sets `zp_eff_poison` (`10 + 1d10` turns).
  - `CHEST_P1_TRAP_PARA ($08)`: Gas trap — paralyzes player (`zp_eff_paralyze`).
  - `CHEST_P1_TRAP_EXPL ($10)`: Explosion trap — destroys the chest (no loot!) and deals **`5d8` explosion damage**.
  - `CHEST_P1_TRAP_SUMMON ($20)`: Summoning runes — summons **`3..5` monsters** around the chest (`chest_summons.s`).
  - `CHEST_P1_TRAP_FOUND ($40)`: Discovered by adjacent Search (`s` / `#` via `chest_search_reveal` in `core/chest_search.s`) or `Find Traps` spell/scroll; enables Disarm (`SHIFT+D` -> `chest_disarm_command`).
  - `CHEST_P1_OPENED ($80)`: Unlocked & opened (`{empty}`, value `0`).
- **Verdict**: **`Implemented`** (100% faithful to VMS Moria / Umoria chest mechanics).

---

#### B. Monster Death & Loot Drops (`monster_death` / `core/monster_death.s`, `core/chest_loot.s`)

When a monster dies in VMS Moria 4.8 (`moria.inc:2319-2344`), Umoria 5.7.15 (`monster.cpp:monsterDeath`), or Moria8 (`core/monster_death.s`, `core/chest_loot.s:180-290`), loot drops are governed by the **7 treasure flags** in `cmove` bits `24..30` (`cr_treasure` bits `0..6` in Moria8):

| Treasure Bit (`cr_treasure`) | VMS Moria 4.8 (`moria.inc:2319-2344`) | Umoria 5.7.15 (`monster.cpp`) | Moria8 (`core/monster_death.s`, `core/chest_loot.s:180-290`) | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Bit 0 (`$01`): `CM_CARRY_OBJ`** | Drops carried/spawned objects (`place_object` if bit 1 clear) | Drops objects (`carry_obj`) | `chest_loot_flags` bit 0: allows non-gold item drops from `pick_item_type` | **`Implemented`** |
| **Bit 1 (`$02`): `CM_CARRY_GOLD`** | Drops gold/gems (`place_gold` if bit 0 clear; if both bits 0 and 1 set, `randint(100) < 70` drops object else `place_gold`) | Identical (`70%` object / `30%` gold when both set) | `chest_loot_flags` bit 1: when both bit 0 and bit 1 are set, rolls `rng(100) < 70` for item vs gold (`core/chest_loot.s:295-330`) | **`Implemented`** |
| **Bit 2 (`$04`): `CM_60_RANDOM`** | `randint(100) < 60`: `+1` drop | `randint(100) < 60`: `+1` drop | `core/chest_loot.s:225-233`: rolls `rng_range(100) < 60` -> `inc chest_drop_count` (`+1` drop) | **`Implemented`** |
| **Bit 3 (`$08`): `CM_90_RANDOM`** | `randint(100) < 90`: `+1` drop | `randint(100) < 90`: `+1` drop | `core/chest_loot.s:235-243`: rolls `rng_range(100) < 90` -> `inc chest_drop_count` (`+1` drop) | **`Implemented`** |
| **Bit 4 (`$10`): `CM_1D2_OBJ`** | `+ damroll('1d2')` (`1..2` drops) | `+ damroll(1, 2)` (`1..2` drops) | `core/chest_loot.s:245-254`: rolls `1d2` (`rng_range(2)+1`) and adds to `chest_drop_count` | **`Implemented`** |
| **Bit 5 (`$20`): `CM_2D2_OBJ`** | `+ damroll('2d2')` (`2..4` drops) | `+ damroll(2, 2)` (`2..4` drops) | `core/chest_loot.s:256-265`: rolls `2d2` and adds to `chest_drop_count` | **`Implemented`** |
| **Bit 6 (`$40`): `CM_4D2_OBJ` / `4d3`** | **`+ damroll('4d3')`** (`4..12` drops in `moria.inc:2340`!) | **`+ damroll(4, 2)`** (`4..8` drops in `monster.cpp`) | **`core/chest_loot.s:267-280` (`bit chest_loot_flags; bvc !cgl_no4d3+`)**: rolls **`4d3` (`4..12` drops)** matching VMS Moria 4.8 `moria.inc:2340`! | **`Implemented`** (VMS `4d3` exact!) |
| **Drop Placement Radius** | radially scatters drops within distance `1..5` around death coordinates (`summon_item`) | Same radial scatter | `monster_death_stage` (`core/monster_death.s:46-59`) queues up to `MD_QUEUE_MAX = 4` kills per turn; `chest_generate_loot` scatters drops onto the death tile and free adjacent/nearby floor tiles (`core/chest_loot.s`) | **`Implemented`** |

| Feature | VMS Moria 4.8 (`values.inc`, `death.inc`) | Umoria 5.7.15 (`data_creatures.cpp`, `scores.cpp`) | Moria8 (`creature_tiers.s`, `core/combat.s`, `core/royal.s`, `core/score.s`) | Verdict | Source Citations |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **The Balrog (`#279` / Catalog `#278`)** | Depth **`100`**, Glyph `B`, Speed `+3`, HP **`3,000`** (`375d8`, `CD_MAX_HP` so always the maximum), AC `125`, Sleep `0`, AAF `40`, XP **`55,000`** (`values.inc:2685-2686`).<br>- **Attacks**: `10d12` fire hit, `8d12` trample, `0d0` eat-charges touch<br>- **Spells (`1 in 3`, `%X'0081C743'`)**: Teleport-To, Cause Serious Wounds, Hold Person, Blindness, Summon Undead, Slow, Drain Mana, Fire Breath<br>- **Flags**: `CM_WIN` + all treasure bits (`$FF` in CMOVE bits 24-31: objects and gold, 60%, 90%, `1d2`, `2d2`, `4d3`) + `CM_MOVE_NORMAL` + `CM_OPEN_DOOR` + invisible, pass-wall, eats others, picks up + `CD_EVIL` + `CD_NO_SLEEP` + `CD_MAX_HP` | Same, as `75d40` (`3,000`) with treasure `4d2` (`data_creatures.cpp:433`) | `MONSTER.CAT` species **`#278`** (`BALROG_CATALOG_ID = 278`, `gen_monster_catalog.py`, Phase 5 `2026-11-16`): Depth `100`, Glyph `B`, Speed code `2` (`+2` Very Fast; VMS `+3`), HP **`3,000`** (`125d24` with `CR_MAX_HP`), AC `125`, Sleep `0`, AAF `40` + `CD_OPEN_DOOR_BIT`, XP **`55,000`** (`$D6D8`).<br>- **All 3 VMS Attacks**: `10d12` `ATK_FIRE` (`ADESC_HITS`) + `8d12` `ATK_NORMAL` (`ADESC_TRAMPLES`) + `0d0` `ATK_EAT_CHARGES` (`ADESC_TOUCHES`)<br>- **All VMS Spells (`1 in 3`)**: `cr_spell_chance` freq `3` + Fire Breath (`HP / 3 = 1,000` unresisted) + Hold Person (`MS_HOLD`); `cr_spell_flags` = `MSF_BREATH | MSF_TELEPORT | MSF_BLIND | MSF_CONFUSE | MSF_WOUND_SERIOUS | MSF_SUMMON`; `cr_spell_flags2 = $1C` = `MSF2_SUMMON_UNDEAD` (`cat_undead[35]`) + `MSF2_SLOW` + `MSF2_DRAIN_MANA`<br>- **All VMS Flags**: `CF_EVIL`, `CD_INVISIBLE ($80)`, `CD_NO_SLEEP ($40)`, `CM2_PHASE ($20)` (wall pass), `CM2_EATS_OTHER ($40)` (`cr_xp` absorption), `CM2_PICKS_UP ($80)`, `cr_treasure = $7F` (`4d3` + `2d2` + `1d2` + `90%` + `60%` objects & gold, plus win trigger in `combat_note_kill`) | **`Implemented`** | `vms-moria: source/include/values.inc:2685-2686`<br>`umoria: src/data_creatures.cpp:433`<br>`moria8: platforms/shared/monster_catalog/monster_catalog_spec.json`, `core/monster_attack.s`, `core/monster_magic.s`, `core/monster_ai.s` |
| **Balrog Death & `Total_Winner` Trigger** | Killing any creature with `CM_WIN` sets `total_winner = true`, prints *"*** CONGRATULATIONS *** You have won the game!"* and *"You may retire (commit suicide) when you are ready."* | Identical (`total_winner = true`) | Killing `MON_BALROG` in `combat_note_kill` / `combat_print_winner_message` sets `GAME_FLAG_WINNER` in `zp_game_flags`, plays `SFX_VICTORY`, and prints *"*** CONGRATULATIONS *** You have won the game!"* | **`Implemented`** | `moria8: core/combat.s` |
| **Winner Retirement Bonus (`kingly`)** | When `total_winner` retires, `kingly()` adds **`+40` to `lev`**, **`+250,000` to `au` (gold)**, and **`+5,000,000` to `max_exp`** (`death.inc`) | Identical (`kingly()` in `death.cpp`: `lev += 40`, `au += 250000`, `max_exp += 5000000`) | **Exact `kingly()` bonus** in `winner_apply_retirement_bonus_overlay` (`platforms/commodore/c64/main.s:2757-2786` and all 7 ports): adds **`+40` to `PL_LEVEL`**, **`+250,000` (`$03D090`) to `PL_GOLD_0..2`**, and **`+5,000,000` (`$4C4B40`) to `PL_XP_0..2`**! | **`Implemented`** | `vms-moria: source/include/death.inc`<br>`umoria: src/death.cpp`<br>`moria8: platforms/commodore/c64/main.s:2757-2786` |
| **Royal Victory Crown Screen (`royal.s`)** | `kingly()` draws the ASCII crown (`#`, `$$$`, `*#######*#######*`) with *"Veni, Vidi, Vici! I came, I saw, I conquered! # 7. Magic System — All 31 Mage Spells & All 31 Priest Prayers

## 7.1 Spellcasting, Prayer, Mana, Learning & Failure Mechanics

| Subsystem / Mechanic | VMS Moria 4.8 | Umoria 5.7.15 | Moria8 Core | Port Differences | Verdict | Source Citations |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Spell Books & Prayer Books** | 4 Mage books (`[Beginners-Magick]`, `[Magick I]`, `[Magick II]`, `[The Mages' Guide to Power]`) and 4 Priest books (`[Beginners Handbook]`, `[Words of Wisdom]`, `[Chants and Blessings]`, `[Exorcisms and Dispellings]`) (`tval = 90` / `91`); plus 17 empty/decoy books in `object_list` (`tval 90..91` with `flags = 0`). | Same 8 active spell/prayer books (`game_objects[395..398]` Mage, `[407..410]` Priest) plus 17 decoy books (`[399..406]`, `[411..419]`). | All **8 active books** are implemented with identical 32-bit `book_mask_0..7` bitmasks (`core/spell_data.s:28-35`, `core/spell_helper_tables.s:21-27`):<br>• Mage books: ID `47` `[Beginners-Magick]` (`$7F,$00,$00,$00`, spells `0..6`), ID `55` `[Magick I]` (`$80,$FF,$00,$00`, spells `7..15`), ID `56` `[Magick II]` (`$00,$00,$FF,$00`, spells `16..23`), ID `57` `[The Mages' Guide to Power]` (`$00,$00,$00,$7F`, spells `24..30`)<br>• Priest books: ID `48` `[Beginners Handbook]` (`$FF,$00,$00,$00`, prayers `0..7`), ID `58` `[Words of Wisdom]` (`$00,$FF,$00,$00`, prayers `8..15`), ID `59` `[Chants and Blessings]` (`$00,$00,$FF,$01`, prayers `16..24`), ID `60` `[Exorcisms and Dispellings]` (`$00,$00,$00,$7E`, prayers `25..30`). (The 17 zero-mask decoy books are omitted). | Identical across all 7 ports. | **Implemented** | `vms-moria: source/include/values.inc:1750-1910`; `umoria: src/data_treasure.cpp:409-435`; `moria8: core/spell_data.s:21-35`, `core/spell_helper_tables.s:21-27` |
| **Max Mana Calculation (`calc_mana`)** | Incremental: `1` when the first spell is learned (`learn_spell` / `learn_prayer`), then `gain_mana(stat_adj)` each level (`misc.inc:2908-2945`): odd levels `0, 1, 1, 1, 2, 2, 3, 4`, even levels `0, 1, 1, 2, 2, 3, 3, 4` for `stat_adj 0..7`, the same two-level rate as Umoria. | `newMana` + 1 (`player.cpp:981-1038`): `levels = lev - first_spell_level + 1`; `levels x 0, 1, 1, 1.5, 2, 2.5, 3, 4` for `stat_adj 0..7`, plus `1` when above `0`; current mana scaled in proportion. | `magic_recalc_mana` (`core/player_magic_tail.s`): Umoria's closed form (`mrm_mana_halves` `0, 2, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8` half-points per level), `0` below the class's first spell level or at `stat_adj 0`; current mana is clamped to the new maximum, not scaled. Creation uses the level-1 values (`create_level1_mana_min` bands). **Fixed 2026-10-26 (M5b):** was `level x stat / 8 + spell_stat_bonus` (`88` at level 10 with `WIS 18/50`, Umoria `21`). | Identical across all 7 ports. Umoria's form, not VMS's per-level increments (Moria8 recomputes from level and stat); mana is granted before the first spell is learned (Moria8 characters start with spells). | **`Implemented`** | `vms-moria: source/include/misc.inc:2908-2945`; `umoria: src/player.cpp:981-1038`; `moria8: core/player_magic_tail.s`, `core/player_create.s` |
| **Spell Learning (`G`) & Forgetting** | `G` (`gain_spells`): mage-type classes choose which spell from a carried book to learn; priest-type classes receive random prayers from carried books. Maximum allowed spells scales with `(lev - min_lev + 1) * stat_adj / 2`. If `INT`/`WIS` or `lev` drops, excess spells are forgotten (`PY_SPELL_FORGOTTEN` bitmask + `spell_order[32]`) and automatically re-learned when capacity returns. | Identical (`spells.cpp` / `player.cpp`). | Full 32-bit `PL_SPELLS_LEARNT_0..3`, `PL_SPELLS_WORKED_0..3`, `PL_SPELLS_FORGOTTEN_0..3`, `PL_NEW_SPELLS`, and `PL_SPELL_ORDER[0..31]` (`core/player.s:68-83`, `core/player_magic_levelup.s:17-206`):<br>• `magic_check_new_spells` computes `mcns_allowed` from `(level - min_level + 1)` scaled by `mcns_get_stat_adj` as Umoria `numberOfSpellsAllowed` (`0` for `<8`, `1×` for `8..67`, `1.5×` for `68..107`, `2×` for `108..117`, `2.5×` at `118`; `18/100` gave `1.5×` until 2026-10-26), capped to `class_spell_total` (`31, 31, 12, 30, 31`)<br>• Pressing `G` (`player_learn_spell` in `OVL_UI`) prompts for a carried book and lets Mage/Rogue/Ranger pick a spell or assigns a prayer for Priest/Paladin. | Identical across all 7 ports (`OVL_UI` / `OVL_SPELL`). | **Implemented** | `vms-moria: source/magic.inc`, `source/prayer.inc`; `umoria: src/player.cpp`, `src/spells.cpp`; `moria8: core/player_magic_levelup.s:17-206`, `core/player.s:68-83` |
| **Spell Failure Rate (`chance`)** | `chance = sfail - 3 * (lev - slevel) - 3 * (stat_adj - 1)`. If `smana > cmana`, `chance += 5 * (smana - cmana)`. Clamped to `[min_fail, 95]%`. | Identical (`spells.cpp`). | `calc_spell_failure` (`core/player_magic_display.s:7-125`):<br>• `fail = base_fail - 3 * (player_lvl - spell_lvl) - 3 * (stat_adj - 1)` (`mcns_get_stat_adj`), clamped to `[5, 95]%`<br>• If `mana_cost > zp_player_mp` (overcasting), adds `+5 * (mana_cost - zp_player_mp)%` (clamped to `95%`). | Identical across all 7 ports. **Fixed 2026-10-26 (M5b):** the stat term was `spell_stat_bonus` (`0..3`, so `WIS 18` took `3` off, not `6`, and `18/100` `3`, not `18`), and fainting from hunger added `+20%`, which neither upstream has. Moria8 clamps a negative chance to `5` before the overcast term, upstream after it (overcasting only). | **Implemented** | `vms-moria: source/magic.inc:30-110`; `umoria: src/spells.cpp`; `moria8: core/player_magic_display.s:7-125` |
| **Overcasting Penalty & First-Cast XP** | Casting with insufficient mana (`smana > cmana`) is allowed at `+5%` failure per missing MP, sets `cmana = 0`, faints the caster for `randint(5 * (smana - cmana))` turns (` You faint from the effort!`), and has a `1/3` (`randint(3) == 1`) chance of draining `CON` by 1. First successful cast of each spell awards `s_exp * slevel / 4` XP (`PY_SPELL_WORKED`). | Identical (`magic.cpp`, `prayer.cpp`). | Fully implemented (`pm_consume_mana` in `core/player_magic.s:590-638` and `pm_finish_success_common`):<br>• When `pm_cost_tmp > zp_player_mp`, prints `"You faint from the effort!"`, sets `zp_eff_paralyze = rng_range(5 * (pm_cost_tmp - zp_player_mp)) + 1`, sets `zp_player_mp = 0`, and rolls `rng_range(3) == 0` (`1/3` chance) to decrement `PL_CON_CUR` (down to min `3`) and recalculate HP<br>• First successful cast sets the bit in `PL_SPELLS_WORKED_0..3` and awards first-cast XP. | Identical across all 7 ports. | **Implemented** | `vms-moria: source/magic.inc:115-160`; `umoria: src/magic.cpp`, `src/prayer.cpp`; `moria8: core/player_magic.s:590-638` |

Every arcane spell (`0..30`) is present in VMS Moria 4.8 (`magic_spell` in `values.inc:2025-2105` / `magic.inc`), Umoria 5.7.15 (`magic_spells` in `data_player.cpp:289-425` / `magic.cpp`), and Moria8 (`core/spell_names.s:25-62`, `core/spell_class_data.s:27-65`, `core/player_magic_execute_overlay.s:97-191`). Each class entry below lists `MinLevel / Mana / BaseFail% / XP_QuarterUnits` (`99/-` = class cannot learn that spell).

| ID | Book | Spell Name | Mage (`Lvl/MP/Fail%/ExpQ`) | Rogue (`Lvl/MP/Fail%/ExpQ`) | Ranger (`Lvl/MP/Fail%/ExpQ`) | VMS Moria 4.8 / Umoria 5.7.15 Effect | Moria8 Effect (`core/player_magic_execute_overlay.s`) | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `0` | Book 0 (`#47`) `[Beginners-Magick]` | **Magic Missile** | `1 / 1 / 22% / 1` | `99 / -` | `3 / 1 / 30% / 1` | `fire_bolt(0, dir, char_row, char_col, damroll(2, 6), spell_names[0])` (`2d6` untyped bolt). | `med_s0`: `eff_bolt` (`2d6` bolt in chosen direction, range `MAX_SIGHT=12`). | **Implemented** |
| `1` | Book 0 (`#47`) `[Beginners-Magick]` | **Detect Monsters** | `1 / 1 / 23% / 1` | `5 / 1 / 50% / 1` | `3 / 2 / 35% / 2` | `detect_monsters()` — reveals all non-invisible monsters on the current level for 1 turn. | `med_s1` (`pmx_detect_monsters_msg`): `eff_detect_monsters` sets `MF_DETECTED \| MF_VISIBLE` on all active monsters without `CD_INVISIBLE ($80)` (`cr_cdefense`) and prints `"You sense the presence of monsters!"` or `"You sense no creatures nearby."`. | **Implemented** |
| `2` | Book 0 (`#47`) `[Beginners-Magick]` | **Phase Door** | `1 / 2 / 24% / 1` | `7 / 2 / 55% / 1` | `3 / 2 / 35% / 2` | `teleport(10)` — short-range random teleport up to 10 tiles. | `eff_phase_door` (`core/spell_effects.s`) -> `find_random_floor_near` (`core/dungeon_features.s`): VMS `teleport(10)`, a random map square pulled halfway toward the player (truncating the step) until its VMS distance (`max + min div 2`) is at most `10`, then accepted if it is unoccupied floor. Moria8 refuses the player's own square and gives up after about `2,000` tries (`1,793-2,048`; `find_random_floor` now shares that budget instead of `200`; upstream loops forever).<br>**Fixed 2026-10-31:** 20 whole-map tries kept only Chebyshev `<= 10`, so the move often silently failed (this row said `±5`). | **Implemented** |

## 7.3 Complete Priest / Paladin Prayer Catalog (All 31 Divine Prayers)

Every divine prayer (`0..30`) is present in VMS Moria 4.8 (`magic_spell` in `values.inc:2106-2184` / `prayer.inc`), Umoria 5.7.15 (`magic_spells` in `data_player.cpp:324-357, 426-459` / `prayer.cpp`), and Moria8 (`core/spell_names.s:64-95`, `core/spell_class_data.s:37-45, 67-75`, `core/player_magic_execute_overlay.s:192-324`).

| ID | Book | Prayer Name | Priest (`Lvl/MP/Fail%/ExpQ`) | Paladin (`Lvl/MP/Fail%/ExpQ`) | VMS Moria 4.8 / Umoria 5.7.15 Effect | Moria8 Effect (`core/player_magic_execute_overlay.s`) | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `0` | Book 4 (`#48`) `[Beginners Handbook]` | **Detect Evil** | `1 / 1 / 10% / 1` | `1 / 1 / 30% / 1` | `detect_evil()` — reveals all evil (`CD_EVIL` / `0x0004`) monsters on the current level for 1 turn. | `ped_s0` (`eff_detect_evil` / `pmx_detect_evil_msg`): sets `MF_DETECTED \| MF_VISIBLE` on all active monsters with `CF_EVIL ($04)` and prints `"You sense the presence of evil!"` or `"You sense no evil nearby."`. | **Implemented** |
| `1` | Book 4 (`#48`) `[Beginners Handbook]` | **Cure Light Wounds** | `1 / 2 / 15% / 1` | `2 / 2 / 35% / 2` | `hp_player(damroll(3, 3))` (`3d3` HP healed). | `ped_s1`: `heal_dice(3, 3, 0)` (`3d3` HP healed via `pmx_heal_and_report`). | **Implemented** |
| `2` | Book 4 (`#48`) `[Beginners Handbook]` | **Bless** | `1 / 2 / 20% / 1` | `3 / 3 / 35% / 3` | `blessed += randint(12) + 12` (`13..24` turns of `+5` To-Hit and `+2` AC). | `ped_s2`: `pmx_add_bless_msg(rng_range(12) + 12)` (`12..23` turns added to `zp_eff_bless`; prints `"You feel righteous!"`). | **Implemented** |
| `3` | Book 4 (`#48`) `[Beginners Handbook]` | **Remove Fear** | `1 / 2 / 25% / 1` | `5 / 3 / 35% / 5` | `remove_fear()` — clears player fear timer (`afraid = 0`). | `eff_remove_fear` (`core/player_magic_execute_overlay.s:367-375`): clears `eff_fear_timer = 0` and prints `"You feel bold."`. | **Implemented** |
| `4` | Book 4 (`#48`) `[Beginners Handbook]` | **Call Light** | `3 / 2 / 25% / 1` | `5 / 4 / 35% / 5` | `light_area(char_row, char_col)` — lights room or adjacent radius-1 area (`2d8` light damage to `CD_LIGHT`). | `ped_s4` (`pmx_light_room_msg`): calls `eff_light_room` (lights room or radius-1 box, deals **`2d8` light damage and wakes** **`CD_LIGHT ($10)`** monsters, `MON-034`) and prints `"The area is lit."`. | **Implemented** |
| `5` | Book 4 (`#48`) `[Beginners Handbook]` | **Find Traps** | `3 / 3 / 27% / 2` | `7 / 5 / 40% / 6` | `detect_trap()` — reveals all invisible traps in area. | `eff_find_traps` (`core/spell_effects.s`): reveals all `TILE_TRAP_INVIS` on the level to `TILE_TRAP_VIS \| FLAG_VISITED`. | **Implemented** |

| `29` | Book 7 (`#60`) `[Exorcisms and Dispellings]` | **Glyph of Warding** | `33 / 24 / 90% / 125` | `37 / 36 / 90% / 50` | `warding_glyph()` — places a Glyph of Warding (`;`) on the player's floor tile; monsters cannot step onto it unless they break it (`randint(OBJ_RUNE_PROT=70) < c_level`). | `ped_s29` (`eff_glyph_of_warding` in `core/player_magic_utility.s:231-249`): places a Glyph of Warding at `(zp_player_x, zp_player_y)` via `glyph_add_at` (up to `MAX_GLYPHS = 4`). | **Implemented** |
| `30` | Book 7 (`#60`) `[Exorcisms and Dispellings]` | **Holy Word** | `39 / 32 / 80% / 200` | `39 / 38 / 90% / 100` | VMS `prayer.inc` case 31: `dispell_creature(CD_EVIL, 4 * lev)` + `cure_confusion` + `remove_fear` + `cure_poison` + `cure_blindness` + `hp_player(1000)`. Umoria `player_pray.cpp`: `remove_fear` + `cure_poison` + heal 1000 + restore all six stats + dispel evil `4 * level` + `turn_undead` + invulnerability to at least 3 (else +1). | `ped_s30` (`eff_holy_word` in `core/player_magic_utility.s`), the Umoria set: calls `eff_remove_fear`, `eff_cure_poison`, restores full HP (`PL_HP = PL_MHP`), restores the stats (`player_calc_stats`), grants `3` turns of `eff_invuln_timer`, dispels all visible `CF_EVIL` monsters for `1..4*zp_player_lvl` damage, and calls `eff_turn_undead_core`. | **Implemented** |

# 8. Ego Items (All 56 Templates) & Complete Base Item Catalog

## 8.1 Inventory, Equipment Slots, Encumbrance, Flavors & Identification

| Subsystem / Mechanic | VMS Moria 4.8 | Umoria 5.7.15 | Moria8 Core | Port Differences | Verdict | Source Citations |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Inventory & Equipment Slot Layout** | `inven_max = 35` (1-based):<br>• `1..22`: carried pack (`a`-`v`)<br>• `23`: Wielded weapon<br>• `24..33`: other equipment (`32`: Outer cloak)<br>• `34`: Secondary weapon (swapped by `x`)<br>• `35`: scratch slot (`constants.inc:79`, `variables.inc:166`, `moria.inc:328-344`). | `INVEN_ARRAY_SIZE = 34` (0-based): `0..21` pack, `22` wield ... `33` auxiliary. | `TOTAL_INV_SLOTS = 33` (`core/item_defs.s:24-50`):<br>• `0..21` (`MAX_INV_SLOTS = 22` pack slots, `a`-`v`)<br>• `22` (`EQUIP_WEAPON`): Wielded weapon or digging tool<br>• `23` (`EQUIP_BODY`): Body armor (includes Cloak `#82`)<br>• `24` (`EQUIP_SHIELD`): Shield<br>• `25` (`EQUIP_HEAD`): Headgear<br>• `26` (`EQUIP_HANDS`): Gloves/Gauntlets<br>• `27` (`EQUIP_FEET`): Footwear<br>• `28` (`EQUIP_LIGHT`): Light source<br>• `29` (`EQUIP_RING`): Ring, the right hand<br>• `30` (`EQUIP_AMULET`): Amulet<br>• `31` (`EQUIP_RING2`): Ring, the left hand (2026-11-08; VMS `inventory[29]`/`[30]`, `moria.inc:528`: a ring goes on the right hand when free, else on the left, replacing its ring; both show as `Ring:` on the equipment screen). Slot `31` was reserved for a cloak slot until then; the cloak will take a new slot (Wave 4.1 catalog in `platforms/shared/item_catalog/` moves `#82 Cloak` to `ICAT_CLOAK (19)` ahead of Wave 4.2/4.3 slot wiring).<br>• `32` (`EQUIP_SECONDARY`): Secondary weapon (SHIFT+X).<br>AC and pseudo-ID cover `22..31` (`EQUIP_END = 32`). Both rings' resistances, See Invisible, Speed, Strength and Slaying count; two Rings of Speed give one level of speed (`PFLAG_SPEED`; VMS adds each ring's `+1`). Each slot stores `inv_item_id`, `inv_qty`, `inv_p1`, `inv_to_hit`, `inv_to_dam`, `inv_to_ac`, `inv_flags`, `inv_ego` (`8 × 33 = 264` B, saved as one block; 31-slot and 30-slot saves still load). | Identical across all 7 ports. | **Approximation** | `vms-moria: source/include/constants.inc:79`, `variables.inc:166`, `moria.inc:328-344`; `umoria: src/config.h`; `moria8: core/item_defs.s:24-50`, `core/item.s:41-53`, `platforms/shared/save.s` `load_read_inventory_state` |
| **Floor Item Capacity & Representation** | Linked/indexed `t_list[1..MAX_TALLOC=225]` referenced by `cave[y,x].tptr` (`1` item per floor tile). | `t_list[MAX_TALLOC=175]`, `1` item per floor tile (`cave[y][x].tptr`). | `MAX_FLOOR_ITEMS = 42` (`FLOOR_ITEM_BASE`, `6` bytes per item = `252` B + `fi_to_hit`, `fi_to_dam`, `fi_to_ac` sidecar arrays, `1` item per floor tile) + `MAX_GLYPHS = 4` (`core/item_defs.s:45-68`, `core/item.s:68-115`). | Identical across all 7 ports. | **Approximation** | `vms-moria: source/include/constants.inc:48`; `umoria: src/config.h`; `moria8: core/item_defs.s:45-68`, `core/item.s:68-115` |
| **Weight & Encumbrance Speed Penalty** | None: VMS refuses an item over the weight limit (`inven_check_weight`, previous section's Carrying Capacity row) and never slows the player for weight | `playerStrength` (`player.cpp:759-775`): over `playerCarryingLoadLimit`, speed drops by `pack weight / (limit + 1)` ("Your pack is so heavy that it slows you down."); nothing is refused for weight | VMS's refusal (`inv_add_carried`, 2026-11-13; maintainer: "the VMS approach on encumberance for now"); no speed penalty. `it_weight` (`core/item_tables.s`, one per item type) holds weights in `1/10 lb` units (`0..255`); Wave 4.1 `ITEM.CAT` (`'ITM2'`) stores lossless `16`-bit weights (`weights` table, fixing `Plate Mail` `380`, `Mithril Plate Mail` `320`, and `157..369`) | Umoria's slowdown is not implemented (maintainer: VMS for now) | **`Implemented`** (VMS) | `vms-moria: source/include/misc.inc:2589-2611`; `umoria: src/player.cpp:759-775`, `src/inventory.cpp:245-257`; `moria8: core/inv_add.s`, `core/item_tables.s` |
| **Unidentified Flavors & Identification (`item_identified`)** | Randomized per-game flavor tables (`magic_init` in `misc.inc`):<br>• `67` potion colors (`colors`)<br>• `25` mushroom/mold adjectives (`mushrooms`)<br>• `25` staff woods (`woods`)<br>• `25` wand metals (`metals`)<br>• `32` ring stones (`rocks`)<br>• `15` amulet materials (`amulets`)<br>• Generated gibberish scroll titles (`syllables`).<br>Identified per item type via `object_ident[1..max_objects]` and per instance via `ident` flags (`ID_KNOWN2`, `ID_TRIED`, `ID_DAMD`). | Identical randomized flavor tables (`data_Recall.cpp` / `treasure.cpp`). | Deterministic flavor tables + per-type and per-slot identification (`core/item_identification.s`, `core/item_defs.s:89-99`):<br>• `item_identified` bit array (`17` bytes = `136` bits, 1 bit per item type ID `0..135`)<br>• Per-slot `IF_IDENTIFIED ($02)` and `IF_CURSED ($01)` flags in `inv_flags` / `fi_flags`<br>• Flavor tables in `core/item_identification.s` assign distinct colors, woods, metals, stones, and scroll titles to unidentified Potions (`ICAT_POTION`), Scrolls (`ICAT_SCROLL`), Rings (`ICAT_RING`), Wands (`ICAT_WAND`), Staves (`ICAT_STAFF`), and Amulets (`ICAT_AMULET`)<br>• Quaffing/reading/using or casting `Identify` (`#20` / Scroll `#21`) sets `item_identified` and reveals `+to_hit`, `+to_dam`, `+to_ac`, charges, and ego suffixes.<br>**Fixed 2026-11-05:** the flavour names carried their own article ("a Blue Potion", "an Iron Wand", "a Scroll of Lumen") where identified names carry none ("Potion of Cure Light Wounds"), so the look and pick-up messages read "You see a a Blue Potion."; the articles are gone ("Blue Potion", "Iron Wand", "Scroll of Lumen", "Cloudy Potion", "Silver Ring"). Wave 4.1 catalog (`platforms/shared/item_catalog/`) prepares `ITEM_TYPE_COUNT = 370` (`47`-B / `376`-bit `id_known_bits` budget) and `FLAVOUR_COUNT = 174` across 5 randomized classes (`46` potions, `49` scrolls, `30` rings, `24` wands, `25` staves — preserving Moria8 `0..11`/`0..11`/`0..3`/`0..4`/`0..4` indices; `flavour_colors[174]` in `item_hot_tables.s`, `flavour_names` in `ITEM.CAT`), with fixed-flavor potions (`#271`, `#273`, `#274`) at `it_unknown_idx = $FF` and `#320`/`#321` sharing `Ring of Adornment` slot `17`. | Identical across all 7 ports (`ItemNameStreams` in Bank 1 on C128, Aux RAM on Apple IIe, Bank 3 on CX16, Bank 6 on BBC Master). | **Implemented** | `vms-moria: source/include/values.inc:1890-1985`, `source/desc.inc`; `umoria: src/treasure.cpp`, `src/identification.cpp`; `moria8: core/item_identification.s`, `core/item_defs.s:89-99` |
| **Inscription (`{`)** | `inscribe_object` (`{`): attaches a 12-character player note to an item. | Identical (`{` command). | Omitted (not in `core/`). | None. | **Missing** | `vms-moria: source/moria2.inc`; `umoria: src/dungeon.cpp` |
