## 8.2 Complete Ego Item Catalog (All 56 `special_item_names` Templates + Moria8 Tool Prefixes)

In VMS Moria 4.8 (`misc.inc:800-1500`) and Umoria 5.7.15 (`data_treasure.cpp:524-544`, `treasure.cpp:35-820`), `special_item_names[0..55]` defines **56 ego/special suffixes** (`0` = `CNIL`, `1..55` = named suffixes). Moria8 implements **8 melee weapon ego types** (`EGO_SLAY_ANIMAL` through `EGO_SLAY_DRAGON`), **5 resistance armour suffixes** (`EGO_RESIST` through `EGO_RESIST_LIGHT`, 2026-11-11; IDs in `core/item_defs.s`, roll and suffixes in `core/ego_items.s`), **2 digging tool ego prefixes** (`Gnomish` and `Dwarven` in `core/game_loop.s:2867-2910`), and **chest lock/trap/disarmed state inspection** (`core/chest.s`).

Wave 4.1 catalog (`platforms/shared/item_catalog/`) expands the data tables to `EGO_COUNT = 55` (`1..55` in bits `0..5` `0..63`, reserving bit 6 `$40` for `page_bit`, plus `0 = EGO_NONE`): Moria8 IDs `1..14` (`EGO_SLAY_ANIMAL..EGO_MAGI`) stay unchanged in-place, and VMS `special_item_names` `15..55` append in `ITEM.CAT` (`egos` + compressed string table; runtime wiring deferred to Wave 4.2+).

| SN ID | Suffix / Prefix Name | Item Category | VMS Moria 4.8 / Umoria 5.7.15 Flags, Bonuses & Cost Delta | Moria8 Implementation (`core/ego_items.s`, `core/game_loop.s`, `core/chest.s`) | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `0` | *(None / `CNIL`)* | All | Standard non-ego item. | `EGO_NONE = 0`. | **Implemented** |
| `1` | **`(R)`** *(Resist)* | Hard/Soft Armor | `TR_RES_LIGHT \| TR_RES_COLD \| TR_RES_ACID \| TR_RES_FIRE`, `+5` To-AC, `+2500` GP (`1/9` of special armors). | **`EGO_RESIST = 9`** (`" (Resist)"`): fire, cold, acid and lightning resistance (`PFLAG_RESIST_*`), `+5` to-AC. **Fixed 2026-11-11 (Balrog queue):** no armour resisted anything. | **Implemented** |
| `2` | **`(RA)`** *(Resist Acid)* | Hard/Soft Armor | `TR_RES_ACID`, `+1000` GP (`1/9` of special armors). | **`EGO_RESIST_ACID = 10`** (`" (Resist Acid)"`): `PFLAG_RESIST_ACID`, and the piece itself takes acid unharmed ("Your `<item>` resists damage!"). **Fixed 2026-11-11.** | **Implemented** |
| `3` | **`(RF)`** *(Resist Fire)* | Hard/Soft Armor | `TR_RES_FIRE`, `+600` GP (`2/9` of special armors). | **`EGO_RESIST_FIRE = 11`** (`" (Resist Fire)"`): `PFLAG_RESIST_FIRE`, as the Ring `#109`. **Fixed 2026-11-11.** | **Implemented** |
| `4` | **`(RC)`** *(Resist Cold)* | Hard/Soft Armor | `TR_RES_COLD`, `+600` GP (`2/9` of special armors). | **`EGO_RESIST_COLD = 12`** (`" (Resist Cold)"`): `PFLAG_RESIST_COLD`, as the Ring `#110`. **Fixed 2026-11-11.** | **Implemented** |
| `5` | **`(RL)`** *(Resist Lightning)* | Hard/Soft Armor | `TR_RES_LIGHT`, `+500` GP (`3/9` of special armors). | **`EGO_RESIST_LIGHT = 13`** (`" (Resist Lightning)"`): `PFLAG_RESIST_LIGHT`, lightning breath `div 3`. Rolled as VMS `magic_treasure` (`roll_armour_ego`, `core/ego_items.s`): body armour and shields with a positive magic bonus, `magik(trunc(min(15 + dlvl, 70) / 6))`, then `randint(9)`: 1 (R), 2 (RA), 3-4 (RF), 5-6 (RC), 7-9 (RL); never in stores (no ego item is), no price change (as the weapon egos). Apple IIe, CX16 and BBC Master print `" (Resist"` then the ego's own 16-byte slot. **Fixed 2026-11-11.** | **Implemented** |
| `6` | **`(HA)`** *(Holy Avenger)* | Swords / Hafted / Polearms | `TR_SEE_INVIS \| TR_SUST_STAT \| TR_SLAY_UNDEAD (×3) \| TR_SLAY_EVIL (×2) \| TR_STR (+1d4)`, `+5` To-Hit, `+5` To-Dam, `+1d4` To-AC, `+10000 + 500*p1` GP (`1/16` of ego weapons). | **`EGO_HOLY_AVENGER = 7`** (`" (Holy Avenger)"`): grants **`×3` damage vs `CF_UNDEAD`**, **`×2` damage vs `CF_EVIL`**, **`+3` AC** when wielded (`ego_ac_bonus[7] = 3`) and, since 2026-11-11, See Invisible (`pf_ego_table`); `3×` base store cost. Sustain and STR not modelled. | **Implemented** |
| `7` | **`(DF)`** *(Defender)* | Swords / Hafted / Polearms | `TR_FFALL \| TR_RES_LIGHT \| TR_SEE_INVIS \| TR_FREE_ACT \| TR_RES_COLD \| TR_RES_ACID \| TR_RES_FIRE \| TR_REGEN \| TR_STEALTH (+1d3)`, `+3` To-Hit, `+3` To-Dam, `+5+1d5` To-AC, `+7500 + 500*p1` GP (`1/16` of ego weapons). | **`EGO_DEFENDER = 6`** (`" (Defender)"`): grants **`+5` AC** when wielded (`ego_ac_bonus[6] = 5`) and, since 2026-11-11, resistance to fire, cold, acid and lightning, Free Action and See Invisible (`pf_ego_table`, `core/player.s`); `3×` base store cost. Regeneration, feather fall and stealth are not modelled. | **Partial** |
| `8` | **`(SA)`** *(Slay Animal)* | Swords / Hafted / Polearms | `TR_SLAY_ANIMAL` (`×2` damage vs `CD_ANIMAL`), `+2` To-Hit, `+2` To-Dam, `+3000` GP (`2/16` of ego weapons; Umoria adds `SN_SA`, VMS Moria had `SN_SM` `(SM)` Slay Monster). | **`EGO_SLAY_ANIMAL = 1`** (`" (Slay Animal)"`, `core/ego_items.s:20, 106, 122-124`): grants **`×2` damage vs `CF_ANIMAL ($08)`**, set from Umoria `CD_ANIMAL` (until 2026-10-25 the generator read `CD_FROST`, so the slay hit frost-vulnerable creatures instead); `3×` base store cost. | **Implemented** |

No `+3`/`+3` on generation, as for every Moria8 ego. The ego field widened to 4 bits in the same bytes: store `si_meta` bits 4-7 (was 4-6), floor `fi_meta` bits 0-2 plus bit 7 for ego bit 3 (both bits were unused, so saved items read as before). C64 `test_ego.s` 11-12. | **Implemented** |

---

## 8.3 Complete Base Item Catalog (All VMS Moria / Umoria Templates & All 140 Moria8 Items)

**Item levels and the picker (maintainer, 2026-11-13):** Moria8's item levels are a compressed
scale, about the VMS level / 4 (0-12, a nibble each). `pick_item_type` (`core/item.s`) puts the
dungeon level on it, `min((dlvl + 3) / 4, 12)`, and its 1-in-12 great item is Umoria's
`level * MAX / randint(MAX) + 1` on that scale. Until then it compared the two scales as one
(`dlvl + 2`), every item could drop from DL10 and a great item drew from the whole pool: a
player found a Mithril Plate Mail (for VMS Ribbed Plate, level 50) on DL8. Eleven items were
raised to their VMS level / 4: Chain Mail 7, Iron Helm 5, Ring of Strength 8, Restore Mana 7,
Protection from Evil 8, Plate Mail 12, Steel Helm 10, Restoration 10, Mithril Chain Mail 7,
Mithril Plate Mail 12, Staff of Remove Curse 12. Item parity with VMS (levels on its 0-50
scale) is the later goal. C64 `test_item.s` 44 and 72 (`DGN-018`).

**Armour AC (maintainer, 2026-11-13):** base AC is VMS Moria 4.8's (`values.inc`). Moria8 had
squeezed it into the low nibble of `it_color_ac` on its own scale (Chain Mail `6`, Plate Mail `9`,
Mithril Plate Mail `14` against VMS `14`, `25`, `28`), so heavy armour gave about half its VMS
protection. Twelve rows changed: Chain Mail `14`, Iron Helm `5`, Large Shield `4`, Scale Mail
`13`, Plate Mail `25`, Steel Helm `6`, Gauntlets `2`, Soft Leather Boots `2`, Hard Leather Boots
`3`, Metal Cap `3`, Mithril Chain Mail `16` (no VMS template; between Chain and Full Plate) and
Mithril Plate Mail `28` (VMS Ribbed Plate). The three past `15` keep a nibble of `15` and
`item_get_base_ac` (`core/item_tables.s`) supplies their values. The worn AC sum saturates
instead of wrapping past `+127`; the `60` cap is unchanged. VMS's heavy-armour to-hit penalties
(`-2`, `-3`) are not modelled. Found with it: `player_calc_combat` rebuilt to-hit, to-damage
and AC from stats and base armour alone, so a level-up, a load, a Potion of Restoration or Holy
Word dropped the weapon's, a Ring of Slaying's and Defender's bonuses until the next wield, and
a load left the AC the monsters read at the saved value. `player_recalc_equipment` now keeps
those bonuses in `pcc_eq_*`, `player_calc_combat` adds them every time, and the load runs the
equipment recalc. C64 `test_item.s` 73 and 74, `test_main_loop.s` 56.

### 8.3.1 Moria8 Master Item Table (All 370 Item Templates, IDs `0..369`)

Source: `core/item_tables.s:13-660`, `core/item_defs.s:1-44` (active `0..156`); `platforms/shared/item_catalog/item_catalog_spec.json`, `item_hot_tables.s`, `item_cat_layout.s`, `item_cat_data.s` (Wave 4.1 full `0..369` catalog + `ITEM.CAT` `'ITM2'`).

**Base costs (fixed 2026-10-26, M5b playtest):** every item that maps one-to-one onto an upstream template (`Implemented` in 8.3.2) carries the VMS `object_list` cost (VMS `values.inc`; Umoria `data_treasure.cpp` agrees except Restore Mana `350`, Chants and Blessings `400` and Staff of Teleportation `800`). Moria8 had its own scale: a Potion of Cure Light Wounds cost `50` (VMS `15`), a Flask of Oil `10` (`3`), the Beginners Handbook `100` (`25`), Words of Wisdom `300` (`100`), Chain Mail `80` (`530`) and a Long Sword `60` (`300`). **Fixed 2026-10-26 (after the byte recovery freed town-overlay room):** the nine that needed a sparse high byte (`it_cost_hi_extra`) now carry the VMS price too: Ring of Strength (`#24`, `400`; was `120`), Wand of Lightning (`#40`, `600`; `200`), Wand of Frost (`#41`, `800`; `250`), Wand of Stinking Cloud (`#42`, `400`; `150`), Pike (`#75`, `358`; `100`), Halberd (`#76`, `430`; `150`), Lucerne Hammer (`#93`, `376`; `110`), Broad Axe (`#94`, `304`; `100`), Awl-Pike (`#95`, `340`; `80`). The worthless upstream items (Poison, Blindness and Confusion potions, Aggravate Monster scroll; VMS `0`) stay at `5`: the stores refuse an item priced `0`. Adapted items (`Approximation` in 8.3.2) keep their Moria8 costs.

**Wave 4.1 VMS 4.8 Catalog Expansion (`0..369`, `platforms/shared/item_catalog/`):** IDs `0..156` preserve all existing Moria8 templates in-place (with `#82 Cloak` recategorized from `ICAT_ARMOR (3)` to `ICAT_CLOAK (19)` and `#121 Staff of Speed` cost set to `1000`), and IDs `157..369` append all `213` remaining VMS Moria 4.8 dungeon & store templates under Base-255 Paged 9-bit IDs (Page 0 = `0..254`, Page 1 = `255..369` via `slot_lo = id - 255`, `$FF` empty on both pages). Hot tables (`item_hot_tables.s`) pack `(color<<4) | min(base_ac, 15)` plus an 11-entry `it_ac_extra_*` lookup for Page 0 armors with `base_ac >= 15`; cold attributes, `16`-bit weights/costs, `32`-bit flags, and Huffman-compressed names pack into `'ITM2'` `ITEM.CAT` (`<= 7,800 B` cap).

### 8.3.2 Complete VMS Moria 4.8 & Umoria 5.7.15 Base Item Catalog (`0..419` Row-by-Row)

In VMS Moria 4.8 (`source/include/values.inc:755-1880`) and Umoria 5.7.15 (`src/data_treasure.cpp:64-522`), `game_objects[0..343]` corresponds 1-to-1 with 1-indexed `object_list[1..344]` in VMS Moria, followed by store-only templates (`344..366`), terrain/trap objects (`367..398`), gold/gems (`399..416`), and special placeholders (`417..419`). Every template is listed below with its Umoria index (`0..419`), VMS Moria 1-based index (`1..344` / `inventory_init` / `gold_list`), category, stats (`Cost / Weight / Depth / Dam or AC`), Moria8 item ID mapping, and verdict.

| Umoria ID | VMS ID | Item Name | Category (`tval`) | Cost (`GP`) | Weight (`0.1 lb`) | Depth (`dlvl`) | Stats (`Dam` / `AC` / `P1` / `Flags`) | Moria8 Mapping (`core/item_tables.s`) | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `0` | `1` | **Mushroom of Poison** | `TV_FOOD (80)` | `0` | `1` | `7` | `food=500`, `flags=0x00000001` (poisons player) | Omitted (represented by `Poison Potion #19`) | **Missing** |
| `1` | `2` | **Mushroom of Blindness** | `TV_FOOD (80)` | `0` | `1` | `9` | `food=500`, `flags=0x00000002` (blinds player) | Omitted (represented by `Blindness Potion #28`) | **Missing** |

As VMS (`scrolls.inc` case 4, `enchant()` `spells.inc:1935`): a cursed worn armour piece first, else one at random among those worn (body, shield, head, hands, feet; no cloak slot); its to-AC rises by one always at `+0` or below, else when `randint(1000)` beats `40, 100, 200, 400, 600, 700, 800, 900, 950` for `+1..+9` and `995` above ("The enchantment fails."); success lifts a curse (`irs_effect_enchant_armor`, `core/item_actions_overlay.s`).<br>**Fixed 2026-11-06 (playtest queue):** it took the first piece under `+5` by an unsigned compare, so armour that acid had worn below zero (`[14,-14]`) was never enchanted; it stopped at `+5`; and a cursed piece was reset to `+0`. Enchant Weapon's `+5` test is signed too since then | **Implemented** |

| `237..239` | `234..236` | **Potion of Cure Light Wounds** *(3 depth rows: dlvl `0, 1, 2`)* | `TV_POTION1 (75)` | `15` | `4` | `0 / 1 / 2` | Heals `2d7` HP + cures blindness | **Moria8 `#17` (`Cure Light Wounds Potion`)**: `cost=15`, `wt=4`, `min_lvl=1`, heals `2d7` HP and cures blindness, all ports. A cure sets a timer above `1` to `1` (VMS `cure_*`), so the effect tick of the same turn ends it with the usual message ("You can see again.", "You feel less confused.", "You feel better."); the heal is VMS `hp_player`: nothing and no message at full HP, else the message for the rolled amount. Identified on quaff only when the heal does something (VMS `ident := hp_player(...)`; Umoria also identifies on a cure) on Apple IIe, C128, CX16 and BBC Master; C64, Plus/4 and Atari 8-bit keep the documented unconditional identify.<br>**Fixed 2026-10-31:** Moria8 healed `rng(8)+4` (`4..11`) and cured nothing (this row said `2d7`). # 9. Monster AI, Monster Spells/Breaths (All 18), Monster Recall & Complete 279-Monster Catalog

## 9.1 Monster AI, Wake/Sleep, Movement, Multiplying, Packs & Wall Movement

| Subsystem / Mechanic | VMS Moria 4.8 | Umoria 5.7.15 | Moria8 Core | Port Differences | Verdict | Source Citations |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Active Monster Table & Per-Monster State** | `m_list[1..MAX_MALLOC=101+]` linked list (`hp`, `csleep`, `cdis`, `mptr`, `nptr`, `fy`, `fx`, `stunned`, `ml`, `confused`, `cspeed`). | `m_list[MAX_MALLOC=125]` with identical fields. | `MAX_MONSTERS = 32` slots × `MONSTER_ENTRY_SIZE = 12` bytes (`384` B in `monster_table`, `core/monster.s:13-47`):<br>• `+0 MX_X`, `+1 MX_Y`: tile coordinates<br>• `+2 MX_TYPE`: active species slot (`0..31` on-demand dungeon species + `32..39` resident town = `40` active slots on all 7 shipping ports; `$FF` empty)<br>• `+3..4 MX_HP_LO/HI`: 16-bit current HP: `hd_num` d `hd_sides`, or `hd_num * hd_sides` for creatures with `CD_MAX_HP` (`cr_speed` bit 7 `CR_MAX_HP`)<br>• `+5 MX_FLAGS`: `MF_AWAKE ($01)`, `MF_CONFUSED ($02)`, `MF_PROVOKED ($04)`, `MF_VISIBLE ($08)` (gated on `(player_pflags & PFLAG_SEE_INVIS) \| zp_eff_see_inv \| zp_eff_invis` when `CD_INVISIBLE ($80)` is set in `cr_cdefense`), `MF_DETECTED ($10)`<br>• `+6 MX_SPEED_CNT`: `-1` slowed, `0` normal, `+1` hasted<br>• `+7 MX_SLEEP_CUR`: live sleep counter (`0..255`)<br>• `+8 MX_STUN`, `+9 MX_CONFUSE`: stun/confuse timers<br>• `+10..11 MX_FLEE_LO/HI`: 16-bit flee HP threshold. | Identical across all 7 ports. | **Implemented** | `vms-moria: source/include/types.inc`; `umoria: src/types.h`; `moria8: core/monster.s:13-112`, `core/dungeon_los.s` |
| **Wake / Sleep Pressure & Group Wake** | If `cdis <= aaf` or monster is in LOS, and `(randint(10) > stl)` while not resting/searching, `csleep -= (75 / cdis)` (or wakes immediately on aggravate/noise/damage). | Identical (`creatures.cpp`). | `monster_wake_check` (`core/monster_ai.s`):<br>• Checks `distance <= (cr_aaf & $7F)` or `MF_VISIBLE != 0` (`monster_in_awareness`); since 2026-10-25 the same test gates every awake creature's turn in `monster_process_one`, as upstream's `(cdis <= aaf) or (ml)` gates all actions (MON-017; before, an awake creature acted anywhere on the level)<br>• If `auto_rest_active == 0` and `rng_range(10) >= mat_player_stealth`, subtracts `mwc_wake_step[min(dist, 6)]` (`8, 8, 4, 3, 2, 2, 1` for dist `0..6+`) from `MX_SLEEP_CUR`<br>• When `MX_SLEEP_CUR` hits `0`, sets `MF_AWAKE` and, if `CF_GROUP ($20)` is set, calls `wake_group_nearby` to wake all same-type monsters within Chebyshev distance `<= 5`. | Identical across all 7 ports. | **Implemented** | `vms-moria: source/creature.inc:100-185`; `umoria: src/monster.cpp`; `moria8: core/monster_ai.s:425-566`, `docs/MONSTER_STATE_CONTRACT.md` |
| **Greedy Pathfinding, Random Movement & Fleeing** | `mon_move` (`creature.inc:1081-1157`): `get_moves` tries up to 5 directional steps toward player; `CM_20_RANDOM (0x08)`, `CM_40_RANDOM (0x10)`, `CM_75_RANDOM (0x20)` roll random movement before normal steps; a creature moves toward the player only with `CM_MOVE_NORMAL (0x02)`; `CM_ATTACK_ONLY (0x01)` creatures only attack when adjacent, and one with neither (Rotting Quylthulg, `0`) never moves. No creature flees by hit points. | Identical (`monsterMove`, `monster.cpp:1167-1240`); the Quylthulgs carry `CM_ONLY_MAGIC (0x04)` and never move. | `monster_process_one` (`core/monster_ai.s:175-336, 575-800`):<br>• Confused (`MX_CONFUSE > 0`) or unprovoked town creatures (`>= 57`) move randomly (`monster_move_random`)<br>• Rolls `CM_75_RANDOM ($20)`, `CM_40_RANDOM ($10)`, `CM_20_RANDOM ($08)` (`cr_speed` bits 3–5) against `74, 39, 19` on `0..99`<br>• Checks `monster_check_flee` (`MX_HP < MX_FLEE`, a quarter of the spawn HP); if below threshold, flees via `monster_move_away`. **Moria8 addition:** neither VMS nor Umoria has hit-point fleeing<br>• Otherwise calls `monster_move_toward` (tries diagonal step first, then randomly alternates horizontal-first vs vertical-first to break corner sticking). `CF_ATTACK_ONLY ($01)` creatures never move, only melee when adjacent; `tools/parse_creatures.py` sets it for every creature with neither `CM_MOVE_NORMAL` nor a random-move bit (**fixed 2026-11-01:** only exactly `0x01` counted, so the Quylthulg, `CM_ONLY_MAGIC`, walked). | Identical across all 7 ports. | **Approximation** (fleeing) | `vms-moria: source/creature.inc:200-450`; `umoria: src/monster.cpp`; `moria8: core/monster_ai.s:175-800` |
| **Door Opening / Bashing (`CM_OPEN_DOOR`) & Glyph Breaking** | Creatures with `CM_OPEN_DOOR (0x00020000)` open closed doors or bash locked/spiked doors; any monster stepping onto a Glyph of Warding (`;`) must roll `randint(OBJ_RUNE_PROT=70) < c_level` to break it. | Identical. | `monster_try_step` (`core/monster_ai.s:801-1268`):<br>• `CD_OPEN_DOOR_BIT ($80)` in `cr_aaf` allows monsters to open closed doors (`TILE_DOOR_CLOSED -> TILE_DOOR_OPEN`) or bash locked/jammed doors<br>• Stepping onto a Glyph of Warding (`glyph_find_at`) rolls `rng_range(70) < cr_level`; if successful, breaks the glyph (`"The rune of protection is broken!"`), otherwise blocks movement. | Identical across all 7 ports. | **Implemented** | `vms-moria: source/creature.inc:460-560`; `umoria: src/monster.cpp`; `moria8: core/monster_ai.s:801-1100` |
| **Multiplying (`CM_MULTIPLY` / `CF_BREEDER`) & Pack Spawning (`CF_GROUP`)** | `mon_move` (`creature.inc:1066-1080`): a `CM_MULTIPLY (0x00200000)` creature that gets a move, while `mon_tot_mult <= 75` and `py.flags.rest mod 7 = 0`, counts the creatures in its 3x3 square (itself included) as `n`; if `n < 4` and `randint(n * mon_mult_adj (7)) = 1` it places a clone on a random free floor tile within 1 square with no object (`multiply_monster`, `moria.inc:2217`). `mon_tot_mult` rises per clone and falls on every monster death. | Identical (`monsterMultiplyCritter`, `monsterMultiply`, `monster.cpp:919-1007, 1171-1177`). | • `CF_BREEDER ($40)` (`core/monster_ai.s`, MON-016): each AI turn, if `zp_mon_count < MAX_MONSTERS - 4` (`28`), `mpo_count_neighbours` counts `FLAG_OCCUPIED` tiles in the 3x3 square (the breeder included) as `n`; if `n < 4` and `rng_range(7n) == 0` (`mpo_breed_odds`), a clone goes on an empty adjacent tile (`find_adjacent_empty`). While auto-rest runs, a further `rng_range(7) == 0` stands in for upstream's every-7th-rest-turn check.<br>**Fixed 2026-10-25:** the roll was a flat `rng_range(12)`, crowding never stopped it and resting did not slow it, so a swarm kept multiplying (M5b: Giant White Mice killed 30 Priests in rounds 28-31).<br>**Deltas:** the 28-monster table limit stands in for `mon_tot_mult <= 75`, which a 32-slot table never reaches (the count can only exceed the bred creatures alive); the clone tile may hold an object; the 1-in-7 rest roll is random, not every 7th rest turn<br>• `CF_GROUP ($20)` (`core/monster.s`): spawns pack members in adjacent tiles on level creation; neither VMS nor Umoria has packs, so no shipped creature carries it (only the embedded test roster).<br>Until 2026-10-25 `tools/parse_creatures.py` never mapped `CM_MULTIPLY`, so nothing bred; worm masses now do. | Identical across all 7 ports. | **Implemented** (breeding, with the deltas above); packs unused, as upstream | `vms-moria: source/include/creature.inc:1066-1080`, `moria.inc:2217`; `umoria: src/monster.cpp:919-1007`; `moria8: core/monster_ai.s` (`mpo_count_neighbours`) |
| **Pass-Wall (`CM_PHASE`), Eat-Other (`CM_EATS_OTHER`) & Pick-Up (`CM_PICKS_UP`)** | `0x00040000` moves through rock (`Xorn`, `Ghost`, `Wraith`, `Spirit`, `Balrog`); `0x00080000` kills/absorbs weaker monsters in path (`cr_xp[mover] >= cr_xp[target]`); `0x00100000` picks up floor items. | Identical. | Implemented in `core/monster_ai.s` (`MON-035`, `TRN-012`, `2026-11-16`) via `cr_spell_flags2` high bits:<br>• **`CM2_PHASE ($20)`**: moves freely through dungeon (`dlvl > 0`) interior walls, magma/quartz veins, rubble, and closed/locked/jammed/secret doors without altering tile/door state (outer map boundary & town `dlvl == 0` walls remain impassable; `mat_force_new_dirty` marks non-local wall cells dirty when entering visibility).<br>• **`CM2_EATS_OTHER ($40)`**: overrides an occupied monster tile when the target is not a win creature (`catalog_slot_is_balrog` / `cr_level >= 100`) and 16-bit `cr_xp[mover] >= cr_xp[target]` (`monster_remove` with no player XP or loot).<br>• **`CM2_PICKS_UP ($80)`**: removes a non-gold floor item (`ICAT_GOLD` and `glyph_*` excluded) upon stepping onto its tile. | Identical across all 7 ports. | **Implemented** | `vms-moria: source/include/creature.inc:480-590`; `umoria: src/monster.cpp`; `moria8: core/monster_ai.s`, `docs/MONSTER_STATE_CONTRACT.md`, `docs/TURN_RENDER_CONTRACT.md` |

## 9.2 Complete Monster Spell & Breath Catalog (All 18 `CSPELL` Flags)

In VMS Moria 4.8 (`creature.inc:700-950`) and Umoria 5.7.15 (`data_creatures.cpp:95-120`, `monster.cpp`), the 32-bit `spells` word encodes cast frequency (`1 in (spells & 0x0F)`) in bits `0..3` and **18 spell/breath flags** in bits `4..23`. **As of Phase 5 (`2026-11-16`, `MON-033`), Moria8 implements all 18 VMS `CSPELL` flags** across `cr_spell_chance` (bits `0..3` `freq`, bits `4..6` breath element `1..6`, bit `7` `MS_HOLD`), `cr_spell_flags` (`8` `MSF_*` bits), and `cr_spell_flags2` (`5` `MSF2_*` bits: `Blink`, `Teleport Self`, `Summon Undead`, `Slow`, `Drain Mana`), casting when `randint(freq) = 1` within `MAX_CAST_RANGE = 8` on clear LOS (`core/monster_magic.s`). Each enabled spell/breath bit is one uniform choice in the cast pool (with Multi-Hued Dragons contributing all `5` breath elements).

| Bit / Hex Mask | VMS Moria 4.8 / Umoria 5.7.15 Spell / Breath | VMS / Umoria Effect & Damage | Moria8 Mapping (`cr_spell_flags` / `cr_spell_flags2` / `core/monster_magic.s`) | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Bit 4` (`0x000010`) | **Teleport Short (Blink)** | Caster teleports itself within `5` (`10` range) squares (`teleport_away(monptr, 5)`). | **`MSF2_BLINK ($01)`** (`monster_cast_blink`, `core/monster_magic.s`, `MON-033`): relocates caster via `find_random_floor_near` (range `10` around caster), updating `FLAG_OCCUPIED` and visibility/dirty flags. | **Implemented** |
| `Bit 5` (`0x000020`) | **Teleport Long** | Caster teleports itself within `max_sight` (`20`–`60`) squares (`teleport_away(monptr, max_sight)`). | **`MSF2_TELE_SELF ($02)`** (`monster_cast_tele_self`, `core/monster_magic.s`, `MON-033`): relocates caster via `find_random_floor_near` (range `60`), updating `FLAG_OCCUPIED` and visibility/dirty flags. | **Implemented** |
| `Bit 6` (`0x000040`) | **Teleport Player to Monster (`Teleport-To`)** | *"casts a spell."*, then the player lands on a free floor square next to the caster (`teleport_to(fy, fx)`). | **`MSF_TELEPORT ($08)`** (`monster_cast_teleport`, `core/monster_magic.s`): *"The `<monster>` casts a spell."* (`HSTR_MM_CAST`), then `find_adjacent_empty_mon` and `eff_teleport_apply_target` (search mode off, full redraw). | **Implemented** |
| `Bit 7` (`0x000080`) | **Cause Light Wound** | `3d8` damage unless player passes saving throw (`player_saves(wis_adj + lev)`). | **`MSF_WOUND_LIGHT ($01)`** (`monster_cast_wound_light`): checks `mm_spell_save` (`wis_adj + zp_player_lvl`); deals `3d8` damage on failed save or prints `"You resist the effects."`. | **Implemented** |
| `Bit 8` (`0x000100`) | **Cause Serious Wound** | `8d8` damage unless player passes saving throw. | **`MSF_WOUND_SERIOUS ($40)`** (`monster_cast_wound_serious`): checks `mm_spell_save`; deals `8d8` damage on failed save. | **Implemented** |
| `Bit 9` (`0x000200`) | **Hold Person (Paralysis)** | *"casts a spell."*; Free Action: *"You are unaffected..."*; else unless saved (`wis_adj + lev`), paralysis `+2` if paralysed, else `randint(5) + 4`. | **`cr_spell_chance` bit 7 (`MS_HOLD`)** (`monster_cast_hold`): *"casts a spell."*; `PFLAG_FREE_ACT` prints *"You are unaffected."*; failed `mm_spell_save` sets `zp_eff_paralyze` to `randint(5) + 4` or `+2`. | **Implemented** |
| `Bit 10` (`0x000400`) | **Cause Blindness** | Blinds player for `12 + randint(3)` turns unless saved. | **`MSF_BLIND ($10)`** (`monster_cast_blind`): checks `mm_spell_save`; sets `zp_eff_blind = 12 + randint(3)` (or `+6` if already blind). | **Implemented** |
| `Bit 11` (`0x000800`) | **Cause Confusion** | Confuses player for `randint(5) + 3` turns unless saved. | **`MSF_CONFUSE ($20)`** (`monster_cast_confuse`): checks `mm_spell_save`; sets `zp_eff_confuse = 3 + randint(5)` (or `+2` if already confused). | **Implemented** |
| `Bit 12` (`0x001000`) | **Cause Fear** | Terrifies player for `randint(5) + 3` turns unless saved or Heroism. | **`MSF_FEAR ($80)`** (`monster_cast_fear`): blocked by `zp_eff_hero != 0` or `mm_spell_save`; sets `eff_fear_timer = 3 + randint(5)` (or `+2` if already afraid). | **Implemented** |
| `Bit 13` (`0x002000`) | **Summon Monster** | *"magically summons a monster!"*: `1` depth-appropriate monster next to the player. | **`MSF_SUMMON ($04)`** (`monster_cast_summon`): prints *"The `<monster>` summons help!"* and spawns a depth-appropriate creature on an open square next to the caster. | **Approximation** |
| `Bit 14` (`0x004000`) | **Summon Undead** | Summons an undead monster (`summon_undead`). | **`MSF2_SUMMON_UNDEAD ($04)`** (`monster_cast_summon_undead`, `core/monster_magic.s`, `MON-033`): picks uniformly across **`cat_undead[35]`** (all `35` VMS `CD_UNDEAD` species in `catalog_index.s`), loads into an active species slot via `catalog_ensure_loaded`, and spawns adjacent to the caster. | **Implemented** |
| `Bit 15` (`0x008000`) | **Slow Person** | Slows player unless saved (`wis_adj + lev`) or Free Action. | **`MSF2_SLOW ($08)`** (`monster_cast_slow`, `core/monster_magic.s`, `MON-033`): `PFLAG_FREE_ACT` prints *"You are unaffected."*, `mm_spell_save` resists, else subtracts `randint(5) + 3` turns from `zp_eff_speed` when unslowed (or `-2` when already slowed, clamped at `-128`). | **Implemented** |
| `Bit 16` (`0x010000`) | **Drain Mana** | Drains `(randint(char_lev) div 2) + 1` mana from player and heals caster `6 * r1` HP. | **`MSF2_DRAIN_MANA ($10)`** (`monster_cast_drain_mana`, `core/monster_magic.s`, `MON-033`): when `zp_player_mp > 0`, drains `r1 = min(MP, floor((rng_range(zp_player_lvl) + 1) / 2) + 1)` MP and heals caster `6 * r1` HP (clamped at `$7FFF`). | **Implemented** |
| `Bits 17..18` (`0x060000`) | *(Unused in VMS Moria & Umoria)* | Unused bits. | Unused. | **Implemented** |
| `Bit 19` (`0x080000`) | **Breathe Lightning** | Radius-2 ball of `mon_hp / 4` lightning damage centred on the player, divided by distance + 1 for monsters in it, no cap; rings and wands on the floor are destroyed (and, in Umoria only, 3% of those in the pack) (`creature.inc` spell 20, `spells.inc` `breath`, `moria.inc` `light_dam`, `misc.inc` `inven_damage`; Umoria `spellBreath`). | **`MSF_BREATH ($02)` + element Lightning (`1`)** in `cr_spell_chance` bits 4-6 (`monster_cast_breath`, `core/monster_magic.s`; 2026-11-08, MON-025): *"The `<monster>` breathes lightning."*, `mon_hp / 4` of the breather's HP to the player, 16-bit and uncapped, through `light_dam`: `div 3` with lightning resistance (Resist or Resist Lightning armour, Defender; 2026-11-11). Not a ball: other monsters, floor items and doors are untouched, and no pack item is destroyed (`docs/BACKLOG.md`). Until 2026-11-08 every breath was fire, `mon_hp / 3`, capped at `255`. | **Approximation** |
| `Bit 20` (`0x100000`) | **Breathe Poison Gas** | Radius-2 ball of `mon_hp / 3` poison gas damage centred on the player, divided by distance + 1 for monsters in it, no cap; no item is destroyed (an empty destroy set) (`creature.inc` spell 21, `spells.inc` `breath`, `moria.inc` `poison_gas`, `misc.inc` `inven_damage`; Umoria `spellBreath`). | **`MSF_BREATH ($02)` + element Gas (`2`)** in `cr_spell_chance` bits 4-6 (`monster_cast_breath`, `core/monster_magic.s`; 2026-11-08, MON-025): *"The `<monster>` breathes gas."*, `mon_hp / 3` of the breather's HP to the player, 16-bit and uncapped, through `poison_gas`: the damage, then `poisoned + 12 + randint(dam)` (8-bit timer, saturating at `255`). Not a ball: other monsters, floor items and doors are untouched, and no pack item is destroyed (`docs/BACKLOG.md`). Until 2026-11-08 every breath was fire, `mon_hp / 3`, capped at `255`. | **Approximation** |
| `Bit 21` (`0x200000`) | **Breathe Acid** | Radius-2 ball of `mon_hp / 3` acid damage centred on the player, divided by distance + 1 for monsters in it, no cap; items of the element's set on the floor and in the pack can be destroyed (`creature.inc` spell 22, `spells.inc` `breath`, `moria.inc` `acid_dam`, `misc.inc` `inven_damage`; Umoria `spellBreath`). | **`MSF_BREATH ($02)` + element Acid (`3`)** in `cr_spell_chance` bits 4-6 (`monster_cast_breath`, `core/monster_magic.s`; 2026-11-08, MON-025): *"The `<monster>` breathes acid."*, `mon_hp / 3` of the breather's HP to the player, 16-bit and uncapped, through `acid_dam`: `mon_atk_acid_armour` (VMS `minus_ac`) damages a worn piece (or a piece that resists acid takes it unharmed) and halves it; acid resistance divides by `3`, both by `4` (2026-11-11). Not a ball: other monsters, floor items and doors are untouched, and no pack item is destroyed (`docs/BACKLOG.md`). Until 2026-11-08 every breath was fire, `mon_hp / 3`, capped at `255`. | **Approximation** |
| `Bit 22` (`0x400000`) | **Breathe Frost** | Radius-2 ball of `mon_hp / 3` frost damage centred on the player, divided by distance + 1 for monsters in it, no cap; items of the element's set on the floor and in the pack can be destroyed (`creature.inc` spell 23, `spells.inc` `breath`, `moria.inc` `cold_dam`, `misc.inc` `inven_damage`; Umoria `spellBreath`). | **`MSF_BREATH ($02)` + element Frost (`4`)** in `cr_spell_chance` bits 4-6 (`monster_cast_breath`, `core/monster_magic.s`; 2026-11-08, MON-025): *"The `<monster>` breathes frost."*, `mon_hp / 3` of the breather's HP to the player, 16-bit and uncapped, through `cold_dam`: `div 3` with the Ring of Resist Cold (`PFLAG_RESIST_COLD`), `div 3` again with the Resist Cold timer (`eff_resist_cold_timer`). Not a ball: other monsters, floor items and doors are untouched, and no pack item is destroyed (`docs/BACKLOG.md`). Until 2026-11-08 every breath was fire, `mon_hp / 3`, capped at `255`. | **Approximation** |
| `Bit 23` (`0x800000`) | **Breathe Fire** | Radius-2 ball of `mon_hp / 3` fire damage centred on the player, divided by distance + 1 for monsters in it, no cap; items of the element's set on the floor and in the pack can be destroyed (`creature.inc` spell 24, `spells.inc` `breath`, `moria.inc` `fire_dam`, `misc.inc` `inven_damage`; Umoria `spellBreath`). | **`MSF_BREATH ($02)` + element Fire (`5`)** in `cr_spell_chance` bits 4-6 (`monster_cast_breath`, `core/monster_magic.s`; 2026-11-08, MON-025): *"The `<monster>` breathes fire."*, `mon_hp / 3` of the breather's HP to the player, 16-bit and uncapped, through `fire_dam`: `div 3` with the Ring of Resist Fire (`PFLAG_RESIST_FIRE`), `div 3` again with the Resist Heat timer (`zp_eff_resist`). Not a ball: other monsters, floor items and doors are untouched, and no pack item is destroyed (`docs/BACKLOG.md`). Until 2026-11-08 every breath was fire, `mon_hp / 3`, capped at `255`. | **Approximation** |

## 9.3 Monster Recall (`/` / `c_recall`) & Full 279-Species Catalog Architecture

| Subsystem / Mechanic | VMS Moria 4.8 | Umoria 5.7.15 | Moria8 Core | Port Differences | Verdict | Source Citations |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Monster Recall (`/` command & `recall[279]`)** | No persistent monster recall database in VMS Moria 4.8 (`/` only identifies the map character class). | Full `c_recall[MON_MAX_CREATURES]` tracking kills, deaths, wake/sleep knowledge, move flags, spells cast, defense flags, and attack slots (`recall.cpp`, `/` command). | Implemented in `core/ui_recall.s` (hosted in `OVL_DEATH` on Apple IIe and BBC Master 128, and `OVL_MODAL` on C64/Plus/4/C128/Atari8/CX16) backed by **`279`-species persistent `recall_*` (`4 B × 279 = 1,116 B`: `recall_kills`, `recall_deaths`, `recall_attacks`, `recall_spells`)**:<br>• Pressing `/` prompts for a monster symbol (`a-z`, `A-Z`, `,`, `$`), lists matching encountered species across **all `279` catalog entries**, and displays a **15-field recall card** (`LEVEL`, `SPEED`, `ATTACKS`, `SPELLS`, `TRAITS`, `WEAK`, `IMMUNE`, `MOVE`, `CARRIES`, `HP`, `AC`, `XP`, `KILLS`, `DEATHS`, `BLOWS`), dynamically pulling core + extended (`MONSTER.EXT`) template fields when experienced. | `recall[279]` (`1,116 B`) lives in C64 `$D8CE-$DD29` (`$01=$34`), Plus/4 `Default`, C128 `Bank1DB`, Apple IIe Aux LC Bank 1 `$D000-$D45B`, BBC Master `LYNNE` `$3F80-$42DB`, CX16 Bank 3 `Cx16AuxData`, and Atari `Default`. | **Implemented** | `umoria: src/recall.cpp`; `moria8: core/ui_recall.s`, `core/monster_catalog.s`, `docs/MONSTER_CATALOG_DESIGN.md` |
| **Full 279-Species VMS Monster Catalog (`MONSTER.CAT` + `MONSTER.EXT`)** | All `279` creatures resident in VAX virtual memory. | All `279` creatures resident in `creatures_list[279]`. | **All `279 / 279` VMS Moria 4.8 creatures playable (`cat_available` all `1`s, `0` degraded, `0` omitted; Phase 5 `2026-11-16`)** across **`29` SoA fields (`57 B/record` = `21` core + `8` extended + `cr_name_lo/hi`)**.<br>• Active level table holds **`40` species slots**: **`32` on-demand dungeon slots (`0..31`)** + **`8` resident Town slots (`32..39`)**, enough for `MAX_MONSTERS = 32` distinct species simultaneously.<br>• `catalog_pick_creature` (`core/monster_catalog.s`) runs VMS `get_mons_num` (`cat_m_level[0..41]`, `1/12` OOD + `randint(4)` up to `DL 40`) and `catalog_ensure_loaded` populates both the `21` core arrays and `8` extended arrays (`cr_atk2_*`, `cr_atk3_*`, `cr_spell_flags2`, `cr_cdefense`) plus the decoded name into `PLATFORM_TIER_NAME_POOL_BASE` (`1,024 B`). | • **C128**: Bank 1 (`128.cat` `$7E60` + `128.cnm` `$A000` + `128.cex` `$D000-$D8B7` under `$7F NOIO`; `MD128` `.crt`)<br>• **CX16**: HiRAM Bank 23 (`MONSTER.CAT`) + Bank 5 (`MONSTER.EXT`) + Bank 4 names (`.zip` & `.crt`)<br>• **Apple IIe**: Aux `$5000` (`A2.CATALOG.PB`) + Aux LC Bank 2 `$D000-$D8B7` (`A2.CATEXT.PB`) + Aux `$8D00` names<br>• **BBC Master**: SWR Bank 6 (`$9500` core + names) + DFS `MONEXT` (`8 B/species` via `< $3000` `OSGBPB A=$03`)<br>• **Atari 8-bit**: `57 B/rec` `MONSTER.CAT` (`140` `.atr` sectors; `130XE` Slots `10..13, 15`; `.car` ROM)<br>• **C64 / Plus/4**: `MONSTER.CAT1-4` + `MONSTER.EXT` (`9` sectors; C64 REU Bank 1 `$2000`; `.crt` ROM). | **Implemented** | `moria8: core/monster_catalog.s`, `platforms/shared/monster_catalog/gen_monster_catalog.py`, `docs/MONSTER_CATALOG_DESIGN.md` |

| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Races & Classes** | `8` Races, `6` Classes | `8` Races, `6` Classes | `8` Races, `6` Classes | **Implemented (`100%` Parity)** across all 7 ports (`core/tables.s`, `core/player_create.s`, `core/spell_class_data.s`). Every stat modifier, hit die, disarm/search/stealth/fight/bows/save skill, XP factor, and class spell-level requirement matches VMS Moria 4.8 / Umoria 5.7.15 byte-for-byte. |

Full percentile `18/xx` range, stat drain/restore (`base` vs `cur`), and racial/class modifiers are preserved on all 7 ports (`core/player.s`, `core/stat_display.s`). |
| **Turn Energy & Speed** | `py_speed` vs `cspeed` energy | `py_speed` vs `cspeed` energy | `player_speed` + per-monster speed counter | **Implemented**. Full speed scaling (`Slow`, `Normal`, `+1` Fast, `+2` Very Fast, `+3` Ultra-Fast) and haste/slow status effects across all 7 ports (`core/turn.s`, `core/monster_ai.s`). |

Preserves Rectangular, Overlapping (80-col), Vault (`RT_VAULT`), Monster Pit (`RT_PIT`), and Monster Nest (`RT_NEST`) rooms, magma/quartz veins, doors, secret doors, rubble, stairs, and traps (`core/dungeon_gen.s`, `core/special_rooms.s`). |

Implements all 18 VMS floor traps (`0..17` in `core/dungeon_data.s`, `core/dungeon_features.s`, `core/disarm.s`, `DGN-019`) and all 6 chest lock/trap bitflags (`core/chest.s`, `core/chest_loot.s`, `core/chest_search.s`). |

Exact per-tile Bresenham visibility with diagonal corner-pinch blocking (`core/los_trace.s`), radius-1 torchlight, and full lit-room reveal (`core/dungeon_los.s`). |
| **Town & Stores** | `6` Stores (`18` owners) + Haggling | `6` Stores (`18` owners) + Haggling | `6` Classic Stores + **Black Market (`#6`)** + **Player Home (`#7`)** + Interactive Multi-Round Haggling | **Implemented + Moria8 Additions**. Preserves multi-round numeric haggling with shopkeeper counter-offers, insults, and 3-insult store kicks (`core/ui_store.s:1057-1502`), and adds a Town **Black Market** (`3×` buy / `0.1×` sell) and **Player Home** (12-slot persistent stash, `core/ui_home.s`). |
| **Combat & Monster Attacks** | `19` Methods, `24` Effects (`4` attacks/monster) | `19` Methods, `24` Effects (`4` attacks/monster) | All `19` `ADESC_*` Methods, all `24` `ATK_*` Effects (`4` attacks/monster) | **Implemented (`100%` Attack Verb & Effect Parity)**. Executes all 4 monster attack slots (`atk0..atk3`), all 19 VMS `ADESC_*` verbs, and all 24 VMS `ATK_*` effects across all 7 ports + 5 cartridges (`core/monster_attack.s`, `core/monster_attack_effects.s`, `MON-032`). |

All 62 spells/prayers are implemented with matching level, mana, failure rate, overcasting faint/CON-drain penalty, elemental `2×` `CD_*` vulnerability / `/4` breath resistance, `CD_LIGHT` `2d8`, `CD_STONE` `100`, and `CD_NO_SLEEP` immunity (`core/spell_data.s`, `core/player_magic*.s`, `core/spell_effects.s`). |

Implements all 55 VMS ego templates (`1..55` in `'ITM2'` `ITEM.CAT` + `core/ego_items.s`, stored in 6-bit `EGO_ID_MASK = $3F` across `inv_ego`, `si_meta_hi`, and `fi_meta_hi`, including `1.5×` `Flame Tongue`/`Frost Brand`/`Fire` ammo vs `CD_FIRE`/`CD_FROST`) plus `Gnomish`/`Dwarven` digging tool prefixes (`core/game_loop.s`). |

Full 370-item catalog (`0..369`) under Base-255 Paged 9-bit IDs (`ITEM_PAGE_BIT = $40`), 16-bit weights/costs, 32-bit flags, 174 randomized flavors (`FLAVOUR_COUNT = 174`), and Cloak equipment slot (`EQUIP_CLOAK = 32`, `TOTAL_INV_SLOTS = 34`). |

Implements all 18 VMS monster spells and breaths across `MS_HOLD`, 5 breath elements (`6` = all 5), 8 `MSF_*` spells, and 5 `MSF2_*` spells (`Blink`, `Teleport Self`, `Summon Undead` via `cat_undead[35]`, `Slow`, `Drain Mana`) (`core/monster_magic.s`, `MON-033`). |

All 279 VMS Moria 4.8 creatures are playable (`cat_available` all `1`s) across all 7 ports + 5 cartridges, with `CM2_PHASE`/`CM2_EATS_OTHER`/`CM2_PICKS_UP`, `CD_INVISIBLE`/`CD_NO_SLEEP`/`CD_FROST..CD_STONE`, full Balrog (`#278`), and `15`-field `/` Monster Recall across `recall[279]` (`core/monster_catalog.s`, `core/ui_recall.s`). |
| **Cross-Port Gameplay Parity** | 1 Platform (VAX/VMS) | Cross-platform desktop C/C++ | `7` Active 8-Bit Ports (`C64`, `C128`, `Plus/4`, `Apple IIe`, `CX16`, `Atari 8-bit`, `BBC Master 128`) | **100% Gameplay Logic Parity Across All 7 Ports**. All 7 ports share the exact same `core/` gameplay, RNG, combat, magic, item, and monster tier tables; ports differ only in map/viewport geometry (`40×25` scrolling on `80×48` map for `C64`/`Plus4`/`Atari8` vs `80×24/25` on `198×66` map for `C128`/`Apple2`/`CX16`/`BBCMaster`), color hardware, sound (`Plus/4` stubbed), and RAM/disk banking. |

## License and source documents

All seven disk ports and the cartridge builds (C64 EasyFlash, Plus/4 `c264 magic cart`,
C128 `Magic Desk 128`, and Commander X16) expose the existing license notice and
source offer from title `A` (About) and Help `L`. Disk builds load the cold
`LEGALUI` modal and documents from program media; cartridges read them from ROM.
The viewer consumes no turns and preserves gameplay state. Ownership and the
change/verification record are in [LEGAL_VIEWER.md](LEGAL_VIEWER.md).
